[\[34a\]]
[\[34a.1\]]rigs kyi bu chos bcu po de dag dang ldan na byang chub sems dpa' smon lam phun sum tshogs pa rnams yin no/_/rigs kyi bu chos bcu dang ldan na byang chub sems dpa' stobs phun sum tshogs pa rnams yin no/_/bcu gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_zil gyis mi non pa'i 
[\[34a.2\]]stobs phun sum tshogs pa rnams yin/_mi tshugs pa'i stobs phun sum tshogs pa rnams yin/_bsod nams kyi stobs phun sum tshogs pa rnams yin/_shes rab kyi stobs phun sum tshogs pa rnams yin/_'khor gyi stobs phun sum tshogs pa rnams yin/_rdzu 'phrul 
[\[34a.3\]]gyi stobs phun sum tshogs pa rnams yin/_dbang gi stobs phun sum tshogs pa rnams yin/_gzungs kyi stobs phun sum tshogs pa rnams yin/_rnam par mi 'khrugs pa'i byin gyi rlabs kyi stobs phun sum tshogs pa rnams yin/_bka' mi gcog pa'i stobs phun sum 
[\[34a.4\]]tshogs pa rnams yin no/_/rigs kyi bu de la ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' zil gyis mi non pa'i stobs phun sum tshogs pa yin zhe na/_rigs kyi bu 'di la byang chub sems dpa' pha rol rgol ba kun gyis zil gyis mi non pa yin te/_de ltar na zil gyis mi non pa'i stobs phun sum 
[\[34a.5\]]tshogs pa yin no/_/ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' mi tshugs pa'i stobs phun sum tshogs pa yin zhe na/_'di la byang chub sems dpa' de'i stobs la gang stobs kyis thub pa'i sems can de lta bu sems can gyi ris na med de/_de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' mi tshugs pa'i stobs phun sum 
[\[34a.6\]]tshogs pa yin no/_/ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' bsod nams kyi stobs phun sum tshogs pa yin zhe na/_'di la byang chub sems dpas ma bsgrubs pa'i bsod nams kyi tshogs 'jig rten pa'am/_'jig rten las 'das pa de gang yang med de/_de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' bsod 
[\[34a.7\]]nams kyi stobs phun sum tshogs pa yin no/_/ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' shes rab kyi stobs phun sum tshogs pa yin zhe na/_'di la byang chub sems dpas de bzhin gshegs pa'i ye shes ma gtogs par sangs rgyas kyi chos gang yang yang dag pa'i shes rab kyis ma mthong ba dang /_
[\[34b\]]
[\[34b.1\]]mngon du ma byas pa dang /_khong du ma chud pa de dag med de/_de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' shes rab kyi stobs phun sum tshogs pa yin no/_/ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' 'khor gyi stobs phun sum tshogs pa yin zhe na/_'di la byang chub sems dpa'i 'khor tshul khrims 
[\[34b.2\]]nyams pa ma yin/_lta ba nyams pa ma yin/_cho ga nyams pa ma yin/_'tsho ba nyams pa ma yin gyi/_byang chub sems dpa'i 'khor ni byang chub sems dpa' mtshungs par spyod pa rnams nyid yin te/_de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' 'khor gyi stobs phun sum tshogs pa yin 
[\[34b.3\]]no/_/ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' rdzu 'phrul gyi stobs phun sum tshogs pa yin zhe na/_'di la byang chub sems dpa' 'jig rten pa'i rdzu 'phrul gyi yul thams cad dang /_nyan thos dang /_rang sangs rgyas kyi rdzu 'phrul gyi yul thams cad zil gyis gnon te/_de'ang rdzu 
[\[34b.4\]]'phrul gyi stobs bskyed pa/_mchog gis zil gyis gnon to/_/de 'dod na 'dzam bu'i gling skra nyag ma gcig gi rtse mo la 'jog go/_/de bzhin du gling bzhi pa dang /_stong gi 'jig rten gyi khams dang /_stong gnyis dang /_stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams kyang 
[\[34b.5\]]de bzhin no/_/de 'dod na gang gA'i klung gi bye ma snyed kyi 'jig rten gyi khams rdul phra rab gcig la 'jog go/_/de bzhin du gang gA'i klung gnyis kyi bye ma snyed dang /_gsum dang /_bzhi dang /_lnga dang /_bcu dang /_nyi shu dang /_sum cu dang /_bzhi bcu dang /_lnga bcu nas 
[\[34b.6\]]brjod du med pa'i yang brjod du med pa'i gang gA'i klung gi bye ma snyed kyi 'jig rten gyi khams rdul phra rab gcig la 'jog kyang rdul phra rab de la gang ba dang /_bri bar yang mi mngon te/_'jig rten gyi khams de rnams kyang phan tshun du 'dos par mi 'gyur/_sems can de rnams la 
[\[34b.7\]]gnod par yang mi 'gyur te/_de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' rdzu 'phrul gyi stobs phun sum tshogs pa yin no/_/ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' dbang gi stobs phun sum tshogs pa yin zhe na/_'di la byang chub sems dpa' 'dod na stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams rin po 
[\[35a\]]
[\[35a.1\]]che sna bdun gyis gang bar byin gyis rlob bo/_/de 'dod na brjod du med pa'i yang brjod du med pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams kyi bar du rin po che rnam pa sna tshogs kyis yongs su gang bar byin gyis rlob ste/_de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' dbang gi stobs phun sum tshogs pa 
[\[35a.2\]]yin no/_/ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' gzungs kyi stobs phun sum tshogs pa yin zhe na/_'di la byang chub sems dpa' brjod du med pa'i yang brjod du med pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing dag na/_sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das ji snyed cig don tha dad pa dang /_tshig dang yi ge tha dad pa dang brjod 
[\[35a.3\]]pa tha dad pa dang /_tshul tha dad pa'i chos bshad pa de dag byang chub sems dpas skad cig yud tsam thang cig gcig la thams cad 'dzin par byed/_khong du chud par byed/_sgom par byed de/_de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' gzungs kyi stobs phun sum tshogs pa yin no/_/ji ltar na byang 
[\[35a.4\]]chub sems dpa' rnam par mi 'khrugs pa'i byin gyi rlabs kyi stobs phun sum tshogs pa yin zhe na/_de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams ma gtogs par byang chub sems dpas byin gyi rlabs kyis byin gyis brlabs pa gang rnam par 'khrugs par byed 
[\[35a.5\]]pa'i sems can de lta bu sems can gyi ris na med de/_de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' rnam par mi 'khrugs pa'i byin gyi rlabs kyi stobs phun sum tshogs pa yin no/_/rigs kyi bu ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' bka' mi gcog pa'i stobs phun sum tshogs pa yin zhe na/_thabs mkhas 
[\[35a.6\]]pa ma gtogs par gang byang chub sems dpa'i bka' gcog pa'i sems can de lta bu sems can gyi ris na ma mthong ste/_rigs kyi bu de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' bka' mi gcog pa'i stobs phun sum tshogs pa yin no/_/rigs kyi bu chos bcu po de dag dang ldan na byang chub sems dpa' 
[\[35a.7\]]stobs phun sum tshogs pa rnams yin no/_/rigs kyi bu chos bcu dang ldan na byang chub sems dpa' ye shes phun sum tshogs pa rnams yin no/_/bcu gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_gang zag la bdag med pa'i ye shes phun sum tshogs pa rnams yin/_chos la bdag med pa'i ye shes phun 
[\[35b\]]
[\[35b.1\]]sum tshogs pa rnams yin/_nyi tse ba ma yin pa'i ye shes phun sum tshogs pa rnams yin/_ting nge 'dzin gyi spyod yul gyi ye shes phun sum tshogs pa rnams yin/_byin gyi rlabs kyi ye shes phun sum tshogs pa rnams yin/_mi 'phrogs pa'i ye shes phun sum tshogs pa rnams yin/_
[\[35b.2\]]sems can thams cad kyi spyod pa la rnam par lta ba'i ye shes phun sum tshogs pa rnams yin/_lhun gyis grub pa'i ye shes phun sum tshogs pa rnams yin/_chos thams cad kyi mtshan nyid kyi ye shes phun sum tshogs pa rnams yin/_'jig rten las 'das pa'i ye shes phun sum 
[\[35b.3\]]tshogs pa rnams yin no/_/rigs kyi bu de la ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' gang zag la bdag med pa'i ye shes phun sum tshogs pa rnams yin zhe na/_rigs kyi bu 'di la byang chub sems dpas phung po rnams skye ba yang yang dag par mthong /_'jig pa dang /_'gog pa yang yang dag 
[\[35b.4\]]par mthong ngo /_/des phung po skye ba rnams snying po med pa dang /_byed pa med pa dang /_gsog tu yang dag par mthong /_phung po 'gag pa rnams 'jig par yang dag par mthong ste/_de 'di snyam du phung po 'di dag ni bdag med pa/_sems can med pa/_srog med pa/_gsob 
[\[35b.5\]]med pa dang /_gang zag med pa yin mod kyi/_'on kyang byis pa so so'i skye bo bdag tu 'dzin pa la mngon par zhen pa 'di dag ni 'di ltar bdag cag gi phung po rnams bdag go/_/bdag cag gi phung po rnams la bdag yod do/_/bdag cag gi bdag la phung po rnams yod do/_/bdag 
[\[35b.6\]]cag gi bdag ni phung po can no snyam ste/_de dag bdag tu 'dzin pa la mngon par zhen pas de kho na nyid khong du mi chud do/_/de kho na nyid khong du ma chud pas mgal me'i 'khor lo ltar 'khor bar 'khyam mo snyam du sems shing /_de thams cad kyang byang chub sems dpas yang dag pa ji lta ba 
[\[35b.7\]]bzhin du rab tu shes te/_de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' gang zag la bdag med pa'i ye shes phun sum tshogs pa yin no/_/ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' chos la bdag med pa'i ye shes phun sum tshogs pa yin zhe na/_'di la byang chub sems dpa' chos la sgro 'dogs pa dang /_
[\[36a\]]
[\[36a.1\]]chos la skur pa yang yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhin du rab tu shes te/_de 'di snyam du glo bur du byung ba'i chos la kun brtags pa'i ming gdags te/_chos de yang de'i ngo bo nyid ma yin/_yi ge de dag kyang de'i ngo bo nyid ma yin mod kyi/_'di ni ming tsam dang /_tha snyad 
[\[36a.2\]]tsam du bya'i/_'di la mngon par zhen par mi bya'o/_/glo bur du byung ba'i chos de yang thams cad du thams cad med pa ma yin te/_gzhan gyi rkyen dang /_gzhan gyi rkyen la sdod pa dang /_gzhan gyi rkyen rnams kyis 'byung zhing rkyen dang bral na 'gag pa'i chos 'di ni yod do zhes de thams cad 
[\[36a.3\]]byang chub sems dpas yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhin du rab tu shes te/_de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' chos la bdag med pa'i ye shes phun sum tshogs pa yin no/_/ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' nyi tse ba ma yin pa'i ye shes phun sum tshogs pa yin zhe na/_byang chub sems dpa' nyi tse ba'i 
[\[36a.4\]]ye shes skad cig gcig la 'jug cing skad cig gnyis pa la mi 'jug pa ma yin gyi/_byang chub sems dpa'i ye shes mthong ba ni skad cig thams cad la thogs pa med par 'jug ste/_de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' nyi tse ba ma yin pa'i ye shes phun sum tshogs pa yin no/_/ji ltar 
[\[36a.5\]]na byang chub sems dpa' ting nge 'dzin gyi spyod yul gyi ye shes phun sum tshogs pa yin zhe na/_'di la byang chub sems dpa' nyan thos kyi ting nge 'dzin rnams kyang rab tu shes/_rang sangs rgyas kyi ting nge 'dzin rnams kyang rab tu shes/_byang chub sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin rnams kyang rab tu 
[\[36a.6\]]shes/_de bzhin gshegs pa'i ting nge 'dzin rnams kyang rab tu shes so/_/des ting nge 'dzin gang dang /_ting nge 'dzin gyi spyod yul gang la nyan thos spyod pa de yang rab tu shes/_ting nge 'dzin gang dang /_ting nge 'dzin gyi spyod yul gang la rang sangs rgyas spyod pa de yang rab tu shes/_ting nge 
[\[36a.7\]]'dzin gang dang /_ting nge 'dzin gyi spyod yul gang la byang chub sems dpa' spyod pa de yang rab tu shes/_des ting nge 'dzin gang dang /_ting nge 'dzin gyi spyod yul gang la de bzhin gshegs pa'i spyod pa de yang rab tu shes te/_de bzhin gshegs pas skabs phye na de bzhin gshegs pa'i ting 
[\[36b\]]
[\[36b.1\]]nge 'dzin ni rab tu shes kyi/_rnam par smin pas grub pa'i rang gi ye shes kyis ni ma yin no/_/ting nge 'dzin gzhan rnams ni rnam par smin pas grub pa'i rang gi ye shes kyis rab tu shes te/_de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' ting nge 'dzin gyi spyod yul gyi ye shes phun sum tshogs pa yin 
[\[36b.2\]]no/_/ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' byin gyi rlabs kyi ye shes phun sum tshogs pa yin zhe na/_'di la byang chub sems dpa' nyan thos kyi byin gyi rlabs kyang ji lta ba bzhin du rab tu shes/_rang sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyang ji lta ba bzhin du rab tu shes/_byang chub sems dpa'i byin gyi 
[\[36b.3\]]rlabs kyang ji lta ba bzhin du rab tu shes na/_sems can de ma yin pa gzhan dag gi lta ci smos te/_de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' byin gyi rlabs kyi ye shes phun sum tshogs pa yin no/_/ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' mi 'phrogs pa'i ye shes phun sum tshogs pa yin zhe na/_'di la 
[\[36b.4\]]byang chub sems dpa'i ye shes ni mu stegs can thams cad dang /_bdud thams cad dang /_nyan thos dang /_rang sangs rgyas thams cad kyis mi 'phrogs pa yin te/_de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' mi 'phrogs pa'i ye shes phun sum tshogs pa yin no/_/ji ltar na byang chub sems 
[\[36b.5\]]dpa' sems can thams cad kyi spyod pa la rnam par lta ba'i ye shes phun sum tshogs pa yin zhe na/_'di la byang chub sems dpa' ye shes yongs su dag pa dang zhing rnyog pa med pas sems can gyi khams la rnam par lta ste/_des de na sems can la la byang chub tu sems bskyed pa dag dang /_
[\[36b.6\]]la la byang chub kyi sems ma bskyed pa dag dang /_la la byang chub kyi sems bsgrubs pa dag dang /_la la byang chub kyi sems ma bsgrubs pa dag dang /_la la sa dang po la gnas pa dag dang /_la la sa bcu'i bar la gnas pa dag dang /_la la mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas pa dag 
[\[36b.7\]]dang /_la la mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas te chos kyi 'khor lo rab tu skor ba dag dang /_la la bya ba kun byas te yongs su mya ngan las 'da' ba dag dang /_la la nyan thos kyi theg pas yongs su mya ngan las 'da' ba dag dang /_la la rang sangs rgyas kyi theg pas yongs su mya ngan las 'da' 
[\[37a\]]
[\[37a.1\]]ba dag dang /_la la bde 'gror skye ba dag dang /_la la ngan 'gror skye ba dag mthong ngo /_/de ltar sems can thams cad kyi spyod pa la rnam par lta ste/_de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' sems can thams cad kyi spyod pa la rnam par lta ba'i ye shes phun sum tshogs pa 
[\[37a.2\]]yin no/_/ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' lhun gyis grub pa'i ye shes phun sum tshogs pa yin zhe na/_'di la byang chub sems dpa' spyod pa 'am/_'greng ba 'am/_'dug pa 'am/_'gro ba 'am/_'ong ba na lhun gyis grub cing ye shes 'byung ste/_'di lta ste/_dper na mi gnyid 
[\[37a.3\]]kyis log pa'i dbugs 'byung ba dang /_rngub pa dag lhun gyis grub cing 'byung ngo /_/byang chub sems dpa'i ye shes de yang de bzhin du blta'o/_/ye shes de yang thams cad du mi chags par 'jug ste/_de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' lhun gyis grub pa'i ye shes phun sum tshogs pa yin 
[\[37a.4\]]no/_/ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' chos thams cad kyi mtshan nyid kyi ye shes phun sum tshogs pa yin zhe na/_'di la byang chub sems dpas chos thams cad mtshan nyid mtshungs par yang rab tu shes/_mtshan nyid gcig par yang rab tu shes mtshan nyid du mar yang rab tu shes/_sgyu ma'i mtshan 
[\[37a.5\]]nyid du'ang rab tu shes/_yang dag pa ma yin par yongs su brtags pa'i mtshan nyid du'ang rab tu shes te/_de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' chos thams cad kyi mtshan nyid kyi ye shes phun sum tshogs pa yin no/_/rigs kyi bu ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' 'jig rten las 'das pa'i ye shes 
[\[37a.6\]]phun sum tshogs pa yin zhe na/_rigs kyi bu 'di la byang chub sems dpa' zag pa med pa'i ye shes 'jig rten thams cad las 'das pa dang ldan pa yin te/_rigs kyi bu de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' 'jig rten las 'das pa'i ye shes phun sum tshogs pa yin no/_/rigs kyi bu chos bcu po de 
[\[37a.7\]]dag dang ldan na/_byang chub sems dpa' ye shes phun sum tshogs pa rnams yin no//_//'phags pa dkon mchog sprin zhes bya ba theg pa chen po'i mdo/_bam po gsum pa/_rigs kyi bu chos bcu dang ldan na byang chub sems dpa' sa dang mtshungs pa rnams yin no/_/
[\[37b\]]
[\[37b.1\]]bcu gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_yangs shing tshad med pa rnams yin/_sems can thams cad kyi 'tsho bar gyur pa rnams yin/_gnod pa dang lan du phan 'dogs pa dang bral ba rnams yin/_chos kyi sprin chen po kun zed pa rnams yin/_sems can thams cad kyi gnas su gyur pa 
[\[37b.2\]]rnams yin/_dge ba'i chos kyi sa bon gyi gzhir gyur pa rnams yin/_rin po che'i snod du gyur pa rnams yin/_rtsi chen po'i snod du gyur pa rnams yin/_mi sgul ba rnams yin/_mi dngangs pa rnams yin no/_/rigs kyi bu de la ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' yangs shing tshad 
[\[37b.3\]]med pa yin zhe na/_rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_dper na sa chen po ni thams cad du yangs shing tshad med do/_/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang 'di lta ste/_bsod nams dang ye shes kyi tshogs kyis kun tu yangs shing tshad med pa yin te/_de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' yangs 
[\[37b.4\]]shing tshad med pa yin no/_/ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' sems can thams cad kyi 'tsho bar gyur pa yin zhe na/_rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_dper na sa chen po ni 'di ltar yo byad rnam pa de dang de dag gis sems can thams cad kyi 'tsho bar gyur pa yin no/_/de bzhin du byang chub 
[\[37b.5\]]sems dpa' yang sbyin pa dang /_tshul khrims dang /_bzod pa dang /_brtson 'grus dang /_bsam gtan dang /_shes rab la sogs pa byang chub kyi tshogs rnams dang /_phan par 'gyur ba'i yo byad rnam pa du ma de dang de dag gis sems can thams cad kyi 'tsho bar gyur pa yin te/_
[\[37b.6\]]de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' sems can thams cad kyi 'tsho bar gyur pa yin no/_/ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' gnod pa dang lan du phan 'dogs pa dang bral ba yin zhe na/_'di lta ste/_rigs kyi bu dper na sa chen po ni gnod pas kyang khong khro bar mi 'gyur/_phan pas kyang 
[\[37b.7\]]chags par mi 'gyur te/_de la gnyis su 'du shes pa yang mi 'byung ngo /_/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang gnod pa dag gis khong khro bar mi 'gyur/_phan pa dag gis kyang chags par mi 'gyur te/_de ni sems can de dag la chags pa dang /_khong khro ba yang mi skyed de de ltar 
[\[38a\]]
[\[38a.1\]]na byang chub sems dpa' gnod pa dang lan du phan 'dogs pa dang bral ba yin no/_/ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' chos kyi sprin chen po kun zed pa yin zhe na/_rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_dper na sa chen po ni sprin chen po las 'bab pa'i chu klung chen po kun zed de/_bzod 
[\[38a.2\]]cing kun 'dzin to de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang de bzhin gshegs pa chos kyi sprin chen po las chos kyi char pa chen po rab tu phab pa kun zed cing bzod la 'dzin te/_de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' chos kyi sprin chen po kun zed pa yin no/_/ji ltar na byang chub sems 
[\[38a.3\]]dpa' sems can thams cad kyi gnas su gyur pa yin zhe na/_rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_dper na sa chen po ni sems can thams cad 'gro ba dang /_'ong ba'i gnas su gyur pa yin no/_/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang sems can thams cad kyi bde 'gro dang mya ngan las 
[\[38a.4\]]'das par 'gro ba'i gnas su gyur pa yin te/_de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' sems can thams cad kyi gnas su gyur pa yin no/_/ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' dge ba'i chos kyi sa bon gyi gzhir gyur pa yin zhe na/_rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_dper na sa bon thams cad ni sa chen 
[\[38a.5\]]po'i gzhir gyur pa las skye'o/_/de bzhin du sems can thams cad kyi dge ba'i chos kyi sa bon thams cad kyang byang chub sems dpa'i gzhir gyur pa las skye ste/_de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' dge ba'i chos kyi sa bon gyi gzhir gyur pa yin no/_/ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' rin po 
[\[38a.6\]]che'i snod du gyur pa yin zhe na/_rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_dper na sa chen po ni rin po che rnam pa sna tshogs kyi snod yin te/_sa chen po la rin po che sna tshogs kun snang ngo /_/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang yon tan rin po che rnam pa sna tshogs kyi snod du gyur pa yin te/_byang chub 
[\[38a.7\]]sems dpa' la yon tan rin po che sna tshogs kun snang ste/_de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' rin po che'i snod du gyur pa yin no/_/ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' rtsi chen po'i snod du gyur pa yin zhe na/_rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_dper na rtsi chen po gang dag nad rnam pa sna tshogs zhi bar 'gyur ba dag kyang 
[\[38b\]]
[\[38b.1\]]sa chen po la gnas te 'jig rten na snang ngo /_/de bzhin du gang dag gis nyon mongs pa'i nad rnam pa sna tshogs pa rnams zhi bar 'gyur ba'i chos kyi rtsi chen po rnams kyang byang chub sems dpa' la gnas shing 'jig rten du snang bar 'gyur te/_de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' rtsi chen po'i snod du 
[\[38b.2\]]gyur pa yin no/_/ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' mi sgul ba yin zhe na/_rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_dper na sa chen po ni 'di ltar sha sbrang dang /_sbrang bu dang /_rlung dang /_nyi ma dang /_sbrul gyis reg pa'i sdug bsngal thams cad kyis mi sgul ba yin no/_/de bzhin du byang chub sems 
[\[38b.3\]]dpa' yang 'di lta ste/_sems can thams cad las byung ba'i phyi nang gi sdug bsngal thams cad kyis mi sgul ba yin te/_de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' mi sgul ba yin no/_/rigs kyi bu ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' mi dngangs pa yin zhe na/_rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_dper na sa chen 
[\[38b.4\]]po ni glang po'i rgyal po dang /[ {]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]kyi rgyal po rnam pa sna tshogs kyi sgra thos kyang mi skrag rab tu mi skrag shin tu mi skrag skrag par mi 'gyur ro/_/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang bdud dang mu stegs can thams cad kyi sgra thos na mi skrag rab tu mi skrag shin du mi skrag skrag par mi 'gyur te/_
[\[38b.5\]]rigs kyi bu de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' mi dngangs pa yin no/_/rigs kyi bu chos bcu po de dag dang ldan na byang chub sems dpa' sa chen po dang mtshungs pa rnams yin no/_/rigs kyi bu chos bcu dang ldan na/_byang chub sems dpa' chu dang mtshungs pa rnams yin no/_/bcu gang 
[\[38b.6\]]zhe na/_'di lta ste/_dge ba'i chos la gzhol ba rnams yin/_dkar po'i chos thams cad skyed pa rnams yin/_dga' ba dang dad pa dang rangs pa'i gsher ba can rnams yin/_nyon mongs pa'i rtsa ba'i rgyud thams cad srul ba rnams yin/_dang zhing rnyog pa med de gsal ba 
[\[38b.7\]]rnams yin/_nyon mongs pa'i yongs su gdung ba rab tu zhi bar byed pa rnams yin/_'dod pa la sred pa zlog pa rnams yin/_zab cing gting dpag dka' ba rnams yin/_mthon dman 'gengs pa rnams yin/_nyon mongs pa'i rdul thams cad rab tu zhi bar byed pa rnams yin no/_/
[\[39a\]]
[\[39a.1\]]rigs kyi bu de la ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' dge ba'i chos la gzhol ba yin zhe na/_rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_dper na chu ni gzhol bar 'gro ba dang /_gzhol bar rgyu ba yin no/_/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang dge ba'i chos la gzhol zhing dge ba'i 
[\[39a.2\]]chos kyi phyir rgyu ba yin te/_de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' dge ba'i chos la gzhol ba yin no/_/ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' dkar po'i chos thams cad skyed pa yin zhe na/_rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_dper na chu ni rtswa dang /_shing gel ba dang /_rtsi dang /_nags tshal thams 
[\[39a.3\]]cad skyed de/_skyes pa rnams kyang phyir zhing rgyas par byed do/_/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang ting nge 'dzin gyi chus byang chub kyi phyogs kyi chos thams cad rnam par byed do/_/skyes pa rnams kyang thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi shing du gyur gyi bar du phyir zhing rgyas par byed do/_/thams 
[\[39a.4\]]cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi shing du gyur pa dag gis sangs rgyas kyi chos kyi 'bras bu rnam pa sna tshogs dag gis sems can rnams 'tsho bar 'gyur te/_de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' dkar po'i chos thams cad skyed pa yin no/_/ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' dga' ba dang /_dad pa dang /_
[\[39a.5\]]rangs pa'i gsher ba can yin zhe na/_rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_dper na chu ni ngo bo nyid kyis gsher ba can yin te/_gzhan yang gsher bar byed do/_/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang ngo bo nyid kyis dga' ba dang /_dad pa dang /_rangs pa'i gsher ba can yin te/_sems can 
[\[39a.6\]]gzhan yang dga' ba dang /_dad pa dang /_rangs pa'i gsher ba can du byed do/_/de la dga' ba ni 'jig rten las 'das pa'i chos tshol ba'o/_/dad pa ni sangs rgyas dang /_chos dang /_dge 'dun la rten pa'o/_/rangs pa ni gang sems dag pa ste/_de ltar na byang chub sems 
[\[39a.7\]]dpa' dga' ba dang /_dad pa dang /_rangs pa'i gsher ba can yin no/_/ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' nyon mongs pa'i rtsa ba'i rgyun thams cad srul ba yin zhe na/_rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_dper na chu ni shing gi rnam pa dang rtswa'i rnam pa'i rtsa ba dag gi rgyun thams cad srul lo/_/de bzhin du byang 
[\[39b\]]
[\[39b.1\]]chub sems dpa' yang sgom pa la gnas pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyi chus nyon mongs pa'i rtsa ba'i rgyun thams cad srul te/_ji ltar na nyon mongs pa'i rtsa ba'i rgyun 'di dag gi ming yang mi shes pa dang /_nyon mongs pa'i dri nga ba'i bag chags kyang med par 'gyur ba de lta bur srul te/_de ltar na byang chub 
[\[39b.2\]]sems dpa' nyon mongs pa'i rtsa ba'i rgyun thams cad srul ba yin no/_/ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' dang zhing rnyog pa med de gsal ba yin zhe na/_rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_dper na chu ni rang bzhin gyis dang zhing rnyog pa med de gsal lo/_/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' 
[\[39b.3\]]yang rang bzhin gyis dang zhing rnyog pa med de gsal ba yin no/_/de la bag la nyal ba dang /_kun nas ldang ba dang bral bas na dang ba'o/_/'dod chags dang /_zhe sdang dang /_gti mug dang bral bas na rnyog pa med pa'o/_/dbang po rab tu dang bas na gsal ba ste/_de ltar na 
[\[39b.4\]]byang chub sems dpa' dang zhing rnyog pa med de gsal ba yin no/_/ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' nyon mongs pa'i yongs su gdung ba rab tu zhi bar byed ce na/_rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_dper na chu ni sa chen po dpyid kyi tsha bas gdungs pa dang /_de las gzhan yang sems can 
[\[39b.5\]]rnams dpyid kyi tsha bas gdungs pa rnams rab tu zhi bar byed do/_/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang nyon mongs pas gdungs pa'i sems can gyi khams thams cad chos kyi chus rab tu zhi bar byed de/_de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' nyon mongs pa'i yongs su gdung ba rab tu zhi bar byed pa 
[\[39b.6\]]yin no/_/ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' 'dod pa la sred pa zlog pa yin zhe na/_rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_dper na chu ni skom pas nyen pa'i sems can rnams kyi skom pa rnam par zlog go/_/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang yul la sred pas nyen pa'i sems can nyen par gyur pa 
[\[39b.7\]]dag la gang gi sems can de dag gi yul la sred pa ldog par 'gyur ba de lta bu'i chos kyi char mngon par 'bebs te/_de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' 'dod pa la sred pa zlog par byed do/_/ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' zab cing gting dpag dka' ba yin zhe na/_rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_
[\[40a\]]
[\[40a.1\]]dper na chu'i 'jings ni zab cing gting dpag dka' ba yin no/_/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' ye shes yang dag par grub pa yang 'di lta ste/_bdud thams cad dang /_mu stegs can thams cad kyis gting dpag dka' zhing zab pa yin te/_de ltar na byang 
[\[40a.2\]]chub sems dpa' zab cing gting dpag dka' ba yin no/_/ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' mthon dman 'gengs pa yin zhe na/_rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_dper na chu'i klung ni sa'i phyogs mthon dman 'gengs so/_/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang chos kyi chu klung gis 
[\[40a.3\]]sems can gyi khams mthon dman 'gengs te/_ji ltar na chus 'gengs pa ltar sems can la gnod pa byed pa de lta bu byang chub sems dpa' ma yin gyi/_sems can de rnams la snying brtse ba'i phyir chos kyi chu klung 'bebs shing de dag la gnod par mi byed de/_de ltar na byang 
[\[40a.4\]]chub sems dpa' mthon dman 'gengs pa yin no/_/ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' nyon mongs pa'i rdul thams cad rab tu zhi bar byed pa yin zhe na/_rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_dper na chus ni sa'i phyogs skam zhing rdul 'thul ba thams cad snum par byed cing /_rdul de 
[\[40a.5\]]dag zhi bar byed do/_/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang sems can sems rtsub pa rnams 'di lta ste/_shes rab la gnas pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyi chus sems snum par byed cing sems can de dag gi nyon mongs pa'i rdul thams cad rab tu zhi bar byed de/_de ltar na byang chub 
[\[40a.6\]]sems dpa' nyon mongs pa'i rdul thams cad rab tu zhi bar byed pa yin no/_/rigs kyi bu chos bcu po de dag dang ldan na byang chub sems dpa' chu dang mtshungs pa rnams yin no/_/rigs kyi bu chos bcu dang ldan na byang chub sems dpa' me dang mtshungs pa rnams yin no/_/bcu 
[\[40a.7\]]gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_nyon mongs pa'i phyag dar khung thams cad kyang sreg par byed/_sangs rgyas kyi chos rnams kyang yongs su smin par byed/_nyon mongs pa'i 'dam thams cad kyang skom par byed/_me'i phung po dang 'dra ba rnams yin/_snang bar gyur pa rnams yin/_
[\[40b\]]
[\[40b.1\]]skrag par byed pa rnams yin/_dbugs 'byin pa rnams yin/_sems can thams cad kyi thun mong du gyur pa rnams yin/_mchod par bya ba rnams yin/_mi brnyas pa rnams yin no/_/rigs kyi bu de la ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' nyon mongs pa'i phyag dar khung thams 
[\[40b.2\]]cad bsreg ce na/_rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_dper na me ni phyag dar khung dang /_rtswa dang /_shing gel ba dang /_rtsi dang /_sdong po thams cad sreg go/_/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang shes rab kyis bag la nyal dang /_kun nas ldang ba dang /_'dod chags dang /_
[\[40b.3\]]zhe sdang dang /_gti mug gi nyon mongs pa'i phyag dar khungathams cad bsreg ste/_de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' nyon mongs pa'i phyag dar khung thams cad bsreg pa yin no/_/ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' sangs rgyas kyi chos rnams yongs su smin par byed ce na/_rigs kyi bu 
[\[40b.4\]]'di lta ste/_dper na me ni lo thog dang /_rtsi dang /_sdong bu'i rnam pa thams cad yongs su smin par byed do/_/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang shes rab kyis nang gi sangs rgyas kyi chos rnams yongs su smin par byed pa'ang ji ltar 'jig par mi 'gyur ba de ltar yongs su 
[\[40b.5\]]smin par byed de/_de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' sangs rgyas kyi chos rnams yongs su smin par byed pa'o/_/ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' nyon mongs pa'i 'dam thams cad skam par byed ce na/_rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste dper na/_me ni chu bran gyi 'dam thams cad skam par byed do/_/
[\[40b.6\]]de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang shes rab kyis zag pa'i nyon mongs pa'i 'dam thams cad skam par byed de/_de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' nyon mongs pa'i 'dam thams cad skam par byed do/_/ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' me'i phung po dang 'dra ba yin zhe na/_rigs kyi bu 
[\[40b.7\]]'di lta ste/_dper na sems can grang ba'i nad kyis nyen pa rnams kyi grang ba med par bya ba'i phyir me'i phung po byed do/_/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang nyon mongs pa'i grang ba'i nad kyis btab pa'i sems can rnams kyi nyon mongs pa'i grang ba'i nad med par bya ba'i phyir shes rab kyis rab 
[\[41a\]]
[\[41a.1\]]tu dro bar byed de/_de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' me'i phung po dang 'dra ba yin no/_/ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' snang bar gyur pa yin zhe na/_rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_dper na gangs kyi rtse mo'am ri bin da'i rtse mo la me'i phung po chen po 'bar na/_des 
[\[41a.2\]]dpag tshad gcig kho ra khor yug tu snang bar byed/_dpag tshad gnyis kho ra khor yug dang /_dpag tshad gsum kho ra khor yug tu yang snang bar byed do/_/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang ye shes kyi snang bas dpag tshad brgyar yang khyab par byed/_dpag tshad 
[\[41a.3\]]stong du yang khyab par byed/_dpag tshad stong phrag brgyar yang khyab par byed pa nas/_'jig rten gyi khams dpag tu med/_grangs med pa'i bar du yang khyab par byas nas/_sems can thams cad la ye shes kyis snang bar byed de/_ye shes kyi snang ba des mi shes pa'i mun pa 
[\[41a.4\]]mun nag thams cad rnam par sel te/_de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' snang bar gyur pa yin no/_/ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' skrag par byed pa yin zhe na/_rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_dper na [{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]khro bo'am/[ {]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]kyi rgyal po khro bos me'i phung po chen po mthong na 
[\[41a.5\]]skrag rab tu skrag shin tu skrag par gyur cing /_'di lta ste/_me'i 'jigs pas skrag pas gnas te/_de ring ba bas kyang shin tu ring bar spong ngo /_/de bzhin du bdud rnams sam bdud kyi ris kyi lha rnams kyis byang chub sems dpa' mthong nas skrag rab tu skrag shin tu skrag par 
[\[41a.6\]]gyur cing /_'di lta ste/_gzi brjid chung ba'i phyir gang du byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi ming yang mi thos na/_mthong ba lta smos kyang mi smos par ring ba bas kyang shin tu ring bar sa'i phyogs de yongs su spong ngo /_/de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' skrag par byed pa yin no/_/
[\[41a.7\]]ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' dbugs 'byin pa yin zhe na/_rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_dper na mi la la zhig 'brog dgon par 'khyams pa las me'i phung po chen po mthong ste/_de de logs su song ba dang /_des de na grong ngam/_rdzi'u brang mthong na de shugs phyin te/_rab 
[\[41b\]]
[\[41b.1\]]tu dbugs phyin nas 'jigs pa thams cad dang bral bar 'gyur ro/_/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang 'khor ba'i 'brog dgon par 'khyams pa'i sems can rnams kyis byang chub sems dpa' mthong na/_shugs phyin par 'gyur/_rab tu dbugs phyin par 'gyur/_

[\[41b.3\]]sems can thams cad kyi thun mong du gyur pa ste/_ji ltar na rgyal po la phan par nye bar gnas pa ltar gdol bu la yang de bzhin no/_/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang sems can thams cad kyi thun mong du gyur pa yin te/_ji ltar rgyal po la phan par nye bar 
[\[41b.4\]]gnas pa ltar gdol bu la yang de bzhin te/_de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' sems can thams cad kyi thun mong du gyur pa yin no/_/ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' mchod par bya ba yin zhe na/_rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_dper na me la ni bram ze dang /_rgyal rigs dang /_
[\[41b.5\]]grong ba dang /_yul gyi mi rnams kyis mchod do/_/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang 'di lta ste/_lha dang /_mi dang /_lha ma yin dang /_dri zar bcas pa'i 'jig rten gyis ston par 'du shes pas mchod de/_de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' mchod par bya ba yin no/_/ji 
[\[41b.6\]]ltar na mi brnyas pa yin zhe na/_rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_dper na me stag chung ngu yang 'tshig pa'i bdag nyid can yin pas na mi brnyas pa yin no/_/de bzhin du mos pas spyod pa la gnas pa'i byang chub sems dpa' mthu stobs ma rnyed pa/_theg pa chen po la las dang po 
[\[41b.7\]]pa yang lha dang /_mi dang /_lha ma yin du bcas pa'i 'jig rten gyis mi brnyas pa yin no/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_lha dang /_klu dang /_gnod sbyin dang /_dri za dang /_lha ma yin rnams kyis byang chub sems dpa' 'di ring po mi thogs par byang chub kyi snying po la 'dug ste/_bla na 
[\[42a\]]
[\[42a.1\]]med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya bar khong du chud de/_de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' mi brnyas pa yin no/_/rigs kyi bu chos bcu po de dag dang ldan na byang chub sems dpa' me dang mtshungs pa rnams yin no/_/rigs 
[\[42a.2\]]kyi bu chos bcu dang ldan na byang chub sems dpa' rlung mar lta bu rnams yin no/_/bcu gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_gnas med par spyod pa dang /_spyod yul kun tu mu med pa dang /_sems can nga rgyal gyis snyems pa'i rtse mo 'thor zhing 'jig la rab tu rnyil bar byed pa dang /_chos 
[\[42a.3\]]kyi char sprin chen po kun nas skyed pa dang /_'gro ba thams cad kyi nyon mongs pa'i gdung ba rab tu zhi bar byed pa dang /_sems can thams cad la chos dkar po lus 'thun par 'byung ba'i dbugs 'byung ba dang /_rngub pa'i srog nye bar sbyor ba dang /_chos kyi char sprin dpag tu med pa kun 
[\[42a.4\]]'dzin pa dang /_chos rnam pa tha dad pa'i gzhal med khang chen po'i bkod pa rnam par dbye ba thams cad rnam par 'byed cing yongs su 'god pa dang /_'khor gyi dkyil 'khor dpag bsam gyi shing las shin tu byung ba'i chos kyi gtam yang dag par rnam par gdon mi za ba'i me tog yid du 
[\[42a.5\]]'ong ba'i char rtag tu mngon par 'bebs pa dang /_bskal pa grangs med par dkar po'i dkyil 'khor yongs su bsags pa'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_rnam par thar pa dang /_gzungs kyi sgo rgya mtsho dang /_ri rab dang /_'od dang /_zla ba dang /_nyi ma dang /_chos kyi tshul dang /_
[\[42a.6\]]nags tshal dang /_khang pa dang /_dge ba'i khor yug gi ri la sogs pas yongs su bskor ba'i sems can gyi 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor na gdul ba yongs su smin pa mang po yang dag par gnas pa bla na med cing lus med pa'i gnas 'pho bar 'jig pa'i ye shes kyi rlung gi dkyil 'khor rab 
[\[42a.7\]]tu gtong ba'i phyir ro/_/rigs kyi bu de la ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' gnas med par spyod pa yin zhe na/_rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_dper na rlung mar ni thams cad du thogs pa med cing gnas med rten med de/_gzugs med kyang nags tshal dang /_khang pa dang /_gzhal med khang dang /_
[\[42b\]]
[\[42b.1\]]ri rab dang /_rgya mtsho la sogs pa thams cad la rang gi dgos pa byed par snang ngo /_/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' rlung mar yang chos thams cad la kun tu mi gnas pa yin te/_phung po dang /_khams dang /_skye mched rnams dang /_gzugs dang /_tshor ba dang /_
[\[42b.2\]]'du shes dang /_'du byed dang /_rnam par shes pa dang /_mig gi khams dang /_gzugs kyi khams dang /_mig gi rnam par shes pa'i khams dang /_rna ba'i khams dang /_sgra'i khams dang /_rna ba'i rnam par shes pa'i khams dang /_sna'i khams dang /_dri'i khams dang /_

[\[42b.4\]]rnam par shes pa'i khams dang /_mig gi skye mched dang /_gzugs kyi skye mched dang /_rna ba'i skye mched dang /_sgra'i skye mched dang /_sna'i skye mched dang /_dri'i skye mched dang /_lce'i skye mched dang /_ro'i skye mched dang /_lus kyi skye mched dang /_reg bya'i skye mched dang /_
[\[42b.5\]]yid kyi skye mched dang /_chos kyi skye mched la mi gnas pa yin/_'jig rten pa'i chos rnams dang /_'jig rten las 'das pa'i chos rnams dang /_lha dang mi'i phun sum tshogs pa thams cad dang /_'khor los sgyur ba dang /_brgya byin dang /_tshangs pa dang /_'jig rten skyong

[\[42b.7\]]dngos po med pa dang /_gcig pa dang /_tha dad pa dang /_de bzhin nyid la sogs par rlom sems kyi rnam pa du mas ma gyur cing dmigs pa med la rnam par thar pa'i sgrib pa med pa'i spyod yul dang ldan pas/_brgya byin dang /_tshangs pa dang /_'jig rten skyong ba la sogs pa'ang byed la/_
[\[43a\]]
[\[43a.1\]]phyogs bcu mtha' yas mu med pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams kun tu sems can thams cad kyi don thams cad thob par bya ba'i phyir phyi ma'i mtha'i mur thug pa'i bskal par thams cad du'ang rgyu la/_chos kyi lus kyis mi snang ba yang yin zhing mi rtog rnam par mi rtog ste/_
[\[43a.2\]]de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' gnas med par spyod pa yin no/_/rigs kyi bu ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' spyod yul kun tu mu med pa yin zhe na/_rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_dper na rlung mar ni phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams dpag tu med pa dag tu reg par byed de g.yob 
[\[43a.3\]]cing rgyu'o/_/rigs kyi bu de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' rlung mar yang chos mu med pa dag kun rdzob dang /_don dam par kun nas mthong zhing rgyas par byed/_reg par byed/_rnam par 'god cing ston te/_de bzhin gshegs pa'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor dag dang /_byang chub sems 
[\[43a.4\]]dpa'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor dag dang /_'gro ba thams cad kyi gnas dag tu yid kyi mgyogs pa dang ldan zhing /_phyogs thams cad kyi mthar thug pa'i spyod yul par gyur nas rgyu zhing 'gro ste/_rigs kyi bu de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' spyod yul kun tu mu med pa yin no/_/
[\[43a.5\]]rigs kyi bu ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' sems can nga rgyal gyis snyems pa'i rtse mo 'thor zhing 'jig la rab tu rnyil par byed pa yin zhe na/_rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_dper na rlung mar ni ri dang /_khyim dang /_nags tshal gyi rtse mo dag 'dud par byed/_rab tu rnyil bar byed/_rnam par 
[\[43a.6\]]'jig par byed/_'thor bar byed/_mi sngar bar byed do/_/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' rlung mar yang sems can nga rgyal dang /_rlom pa dang /_dregs pa dang /_'jig tshogs la lta ba'i rtse mos snyems pa rnams gzugs dang /_dar la bab pa dang /_longs spyod dang /_dbang 
[\[43a.7\]]dang /_stobs dang /_tshe dang /_nad med pa dang /_'tsho ba dang /_bzo dang /_thos pa dang /_yid gzhungs pa dang /_g.yog dang /_smra mkhas pa dang /_dpal che ba'i dregs pas myos pa dag dang /_yang dag pa'i nan tan gyis bdag bstod pa rnams la de dang khyad zhugs pa'i phun sum 
[\[43b\]]
[\[43b.1\]]tshogs pa bstan pa dang /_chos bstan pas nga rgyal choms par byed cing dge ba dri ma med pa la sbyor bar byed de/_de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' sems can nga rgyal kyis snyems pa'i rtse mo 'thor zhing 'jig la rab tu rnyil bar byed pa yin no/_/rigs kyi bu ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' 
[\[43b.2\]]chos kyi char sprin chen po kun nas skyed pa yin zhe na/_rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_dper na rlung mar ni phyogs bzhi nas sprin gyi dkyil 'khor kha dog sna tshogs can dang /_zab cing gsal la mnyen zhing 'jam pa'i 'brug sgra rgya mtsho'i dbyangs can/_klung ro sna tshogs kyi sgra 'byin cing yid du 
[\[43b.3\]]'ong ba'i dbyangs rjes su grag pa/_glog gi phreng ba'i rgyan gyis rnam par brgyan pa gang dag gis 'jig rten gyi khams bye ba khrag khrig stong phrag brgya dag kun nas yongs su khebs par byas te/_'gro ba thams cad dga' ba dang /_lo tog dang /_sdong po yongs su bskyed pa'i phyir 
[\[43b.4\]]char gyi rgyun chen po rtag tu rgyun mi chad par mngon par 'bebs pa'i sprin chen po kun nas slong ngo /_/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' rlung mar yang lus sna tshogs kyi sprin sems can thams cad dga' bar byed pa/_phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams mtha' yas mu med pa snang bar 
[\[43b.5\]]byed pa/_'od zer kha dog mtha' yas pa'i glog gi phreng ba'i rgyun 'byung ba/_yang dag pa de kho na nyid kyi chos kyi 'brug sgra yan lag stong phrag drug cu pa sgrogs pa/_snying rje chen po'i rlung mar gyis yang dag par mtho ba/_chos kyi dbyings kyis klas pa'i 'gro ba thams cad kyi nam mkha' khyab par 
[\[43b.6\]]byed pa dag gis 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kun nas khebs par byas shing ngan song dang mi khom pa la sogs pa nas sems can rnams rnam par bslang ste/_rnam pa thams cad du mtshan dang /_dpe byad bzang po dang /_'od kyi dkyil 'khor snang bar rnam par dag pa yang byin gyis brlabs te/_

[\[44a.1\]]rtsi chen po dang /_sdong po thams cad rnam par bskyed pa dang /_thams cad du skyid par chos kyi dbang bskur ba'i phyir sems can thams cad kyi phun sum tshogs pa dang /_dga' bar byed pa dang /_rgud pa rab tu zhi bar byed pa'i chos kyi char chen po mngon par 'bebs te/_

[\[44a.3\]]snying po can ldang na/_sems can rnams kyi gdung ba yongs su zhi bar byas shing /_lus shin tu sim pa dang /_bsil bar byed do/_/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' rlung mar yang sems can rnams kyi 'dod chags dang /_zhe sdang dang /_gti mug dang /_ngan 'gro dang /_ngan par 
[\[44a.4\]]lta ba dang /_nyes byas dang /_the tsom dang /_phongs pa mi bzad pa dang /_yul la sred pa dang /_sdug pa dang /_mi sdug pa dang bral zhing phrad pa dang /_nad rnams bcil nas dam pa ma yin pa'i chos la sred pa la sogs pa'i gdung ba dag dam pa'i chos kyi char dang 'dres pa'i bla na med 
[\[44a.5\]]pa'i tshul khrims rnam par dag pa dang /_smon lam dang /_mthong ba dang /_reg pa dang /_'grogs pa dang /_bstan pa don yod pa la sogs pas rab tu zhi bar byed de/_rigs kyi bu de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' 'gro ba thams cad kyi nyon mongs pa'i gdung ba rab tu zhi bar byed 
[\[44a.6\]]cing nyon mongs pa med pa'i sa la 'god pa yin no/_/rigs kyi bu ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' sems can thams cad la chos dkar po las 'thun par byung ba'i dbugs 'byung ba dang /_rngub pa'i srog nye bar sbyor ba yin zhe na/_rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_dper na dbugs 'byung ba dang /_
[\[44a.7\]]rngub pa'i rlung gi mthus sems can thams cad 'tsho'o/_/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpas mngon par bsgrubs pa dang /_byang chub sems dpas bstabs pa'i chos dkar po'i rlung mar nye bar spyad pa phun sum tshogs pa thams cad skyed pas kyang sems can thams cad dga' bar 'gyur ro/_/
[\[44b\]]
[\[44b.1\]]rigs kyi bu gzhan yang 'di lta ste/_dper na rlung mar ni 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du 'di lta ste/_rdo rje'i dkyil 'khor rnams dang /_khor yug ri dag dang /_khor yug gi ri chen po rnams dang /_gling chen po bzhi yongs su 'dzin pa'i gser gyi dkyil 'khor rnams dang /_
[\[44b.2\]]rgya mtsho rin po che'i 'byung gnas dang bcas pa rnams dang /_ri rab dang /_ri rab chen po dang /_de ma yin pa'i rin po che'i ri dang /_gangs dang /_ri spos kyi ngad ldang ba dang /_grong rdal dang /_nags tshal dang /_gzhal med khang dag dang /_'dzam bu'i gling dag dang /_gling chen 
[\[44b.3\]]po bzhi dag dang /_stong dag dang /_stong gnyis pa dag dang /_stong gsum gyi stong chen po dag bkod pa rnam par phye ba sna tshogs mngon par sgrub bo/_/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' rlung mar yang phyogs bcu kun tu sems can thams cad kyi bsod nams kyi phung po phun sum 
[\[44b.4\]]tshogs pa nye bar sgrub bo/_/skyed do/_/yongs su gzhog go/_/de la ji ltar gangs kyi ri phun sum tshogs pa de ltar ni 'jig rten pa'i bsod nams phun sum tshogs pa rnams blta'o/_/ji ltar gling chen po bzhi dang ri rab phun sum tshogs pa de ltar ni nyan thos kyi phun sum tshogs pa 
[\[44b.5\]]rnams blta'o/_/ji ltar stong gi 'jig rten gyi khams phun sum tshogs pa de ltar ni rang sangs rgyas kyi phun sum tshogs pa rnams blta'o/_/ji ltar stong gnyis kyi 'jig rten gyi khams phun sum tshogs pa de ltar ni byang chub sems dpa'i phun sum tshogs pa rnams blta'o/_/
[\[44b.6\]]ji ltar stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams phun sum tshogs pa de ltar ni 'jig rten thams cad las mngon par 'phags pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kyi nam mkha'i dbyings kyi mthas gtugs pa/_shin tu rnam par dag par grags pa mchod pa la dmigs pa

[\[45a\]]
[\[45a.1\]]gling dang /_bar gyi gling dang /_ri gzhan dang /_gling chen po bzhi pa la sogs pa ltar ni sbyor ba dang /_gzungs dang /_sems can yongs su smin par bya ba dang /_slob ma'i dge 'dun la sogs pa blta'o/_/grong rdal dang /_nags tshal dang /_gzhal

[\[45a.3\]]byang chub sems dpa' dang yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi stobs dang /_mi 'jigs pa dang /_ma 'dres pa dang /_snying rje chen po la sogs pa dbang sgyur ba dam pa phun sum tshogs pa'i chos rnams blta'o/_/rigs kyi bu de la sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi bsod nams 
[\[45a.4\]]brgya ba'i mtshan gang zhe na/_rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_dper na yongs su brtag pa bzung ste/_phyogs bcu'i phyogs re re'i 'jig rten gyi khams gang gA'i klung grangs med pa'i bye ma snyed dag na sems can gang ji snyed yod pa de dag thams cad stong gsum phrag bcu'i 'khor los sgyur ba'i bsod 
[\[45a.5\]]nams kyi phung po dang ldan par gyur la/_de lta bu'i 'khor los sgyur ba'i dngos po nyid dang ldan pa'i sems can de dag gi bsod nams kyi phung po ji tsam pa de bsdoms pa dang /_'khor los sgyur ba chen po gcig cig ldan par gyur te/_rnam grangs des shar phyogs su 'jig rten gyi khams 
[\[45a.6\]]thams cad 'das te/_de snyed kyi 'jig rten gyi khams su gtogs pa'i sems can de snyed cig 'khor los sgyur ba'i dngos po de 'dra ba nyid dang so sor ldan par gyur la/_snga ma bzhin du phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad nam mkha'i mthas gtugs pa dag na sems can yod do 
[\[45a.7\]]cog ji snyed pa de dag thams cad 'khor los sgyur ba'i dngos po de lta bu sha stag dang so sor ldan par gyur te/_de bzhin du bgrang ba de nyid kyis 'jig rten gyi khams de snyed du gtogs pa'i sems can rnams brgya byin gyi bsod nams kyi phung po dang ldan pa'i brgya byin sha stag tu gyur la/_phyogs 
[\[45b\]]
[\[45b.1\]]bcu'i phyogs re re'i 'jig rten gyi khams gang gA'i klung grangs med pa'i bye ma snyed du gtogs pa'i brgya byin rnams kyi bsod nams kyi phung po ji tsam pa de dang brgya byin gcig cig ldan par gyur te/_de bzhin du 'jig rten gyi khams chos kyi dbyings kyis klas pa nam mkha'i mthas gtugs

[\[45b.6\]]mngon par 'grub bo/_/ba spu'i khung bu thams cad du gtogs pa'i bsod nams kyi phung po brgya stong grangs med pa bcur bsgyur bas/_dpe byad bzang po brgyad cu las dpe byad bzang po re re mngon par 'grub bo/_/dpe byad bzang por gtogs pa'i bsod nams kyi phung po de thams cad brjod du

[\[46a\]]
[\[46a.1\]]pa mngon par 'grub bo/_/de yang brjod du med pa bye ba stong phrag bcur bsgyur bas de bzhin gshegs pa'i spyi gtsug 'gro ba thams cad las mngon par 'phags shing bltar mi snang ba gtsug tor gyis yongs su brgyan pa mngon par 'grub bo/_/de yang brjod du med 
[\[46a.2\]]pa'i yang brjod du med pa bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong phrag bcur bsgyur bas de bzhin gshegs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi gsung yan lag stong phrag drug cu pa dmigs pa thams cad las gsung gi sgra mtha' yas par 'byung ba lhun gyis grub pas sems can thams cad bsam pa yongs su sim par 
[\[46a.3\]]gyur la/_'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du go bar byed pa mngon par 'grub ste/_rigs kyi bu 'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi bsod nams brgya pa'i mtshan ces bya'o/_/rigs kyi bu de ltar na de bzhin gshegs pa bsod nams dang ye shes kyi tshogs mi zad pa 
[\[46a.4\]]dang ldan zhing kun tu rgyas par byed pa bkod pa dang ldan pas sems can thams cad nye bar 'tsho ba yin te/_rigs kyi bu yongs su brtag pa bzung na/_gal te phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams chos kyi dbyings kyis klas pa nam mkha'i dbyings kyi mthas gtugs pa thams cad du 
[\[46a.5\]]gtogs pa'i sems can rnams sa bcu pa chos kyi sprin la gnas pa/_rnam pa thams cad kyi mchog dang ldan pa'i lus dang /_ngag dang /_yid kyi rgyan 'chang ba/_dbang bcu la dbang sgyur ba sha stag tu gyur te/_de bzhin gshegs pa'i ba spu'i khung bu gcig tu gtogs pa'i 
[\[46a.6\]]bsod nams kyi phung po la 'dzam bu'i chu bo'i gser las byas pa'i snod rin po che sna tshogs rnam pa sna tshogs kyis spras pa nam mkha'i tshad tsam gang gA'i klung gi bye ma snyed so so nas thogs te/_phyi ma'i mthar thug pa'i bskal par yongs su bkang zhing skad cig skad cig tu blangs shing 
[\[46a.7\]]khyer yang ba spu'i khung bu gcig tu gtogs pa'i bsod nams kyi phung po de gang ba'am 'bri bar mi mngon te/_rigs kyi bu de ltar de bzhin gshegs pa'i bsod nams brgya'i mtshan ni bsam gyis mi khyab bo/_/de la sa bcu gnyis gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_byang chub kyi sems ma bskyed pa'i 
[\[46b\]]
[\[46b.1\]]sa dang /_rab tu dga' ba dang /_dri ma med pa dang /_'od byed pa dang /_'od 'phro ba can dang /_shin tu dka' rgyal dang /_mngon du gyur pa dang /_ring du song ba dang /_mi g.yo ba dang /_legs pa'i blo gros dang /_chos kyi sprin dang /_sangs rgyas kyi sa kun tu 'od de/_sa bcu

[\[46b.4\]]'khor dang ldan pa/_sgrib pa med pa'i stobs kyi shugs 'chang ba/_'jig rten gyi khams grangs med par skad cig re re la 'gro zhing 'ong na dri zhim po dang /_'od zer gyi dra ba'i tshogs chen po gling chen po bzhi pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams kun tu 'byung ba'i pad mas rkang pa yongs su brtan pa/_stong gi 'jig 
[\[46b.5\]]rten gyi khams kyi mtha' dang mnyam pa'i stan gyi rgyan dang ldan pa/_chos thams cad shin tu rnam par non pa'i lhag mthong rnam par bsgoms pas thogs pa med cing yid du 'ong ba'i dmigs pa'i mtshan ma chen po bcu la sogs pa grangs med pa'i ltas bzang po mthong ba/_phyir mi ldog pa/_phyogs

[\[46b.7\]]mnod pa'i dbang dang byin gyi rlabs la mkhas pa/_'jig rten pa dang /_'jig rten las 'das pa'i chos rin po che'i char gyi snang ba'i sgo dpag tu med pa thams cad gtan pa med par kun tu rgyas par 'gengs pa'i mchod sbyin chen po'i sprin gtong ba lhur byed pa/_blta na bzang zhing yid du

[\[47a.1\]]'ong la mdzes shing dge la 'gro ba thams cad dang mi 'thun pa med cing don yod par bsam pa thams cad yongs su rdzogs par byed pa 'jig rten gyi khams grangs med pa rab tu g.yo bar byed cing 'gro ba ngan 'gro la sogs pa las sems can mtha' yas pa yongs su skyob pa/_

[\[47a.3\]]bskal pa'i mthar thug par rnam par mi rtog cing lhun gyis grub pa'i dga' ba'i shugs kyi snang ba 'thob pa/_bskal pa bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong ther 'bum dpag tu med par theg pa chen po la zhugs pa'i bsgom pa sna tshogs yongs su rdzogs la/_gzhan gyi don gyi nan tan tshar phyin pa'i

[\[47a.6\]]byangachub sems dpa'i sa ni ma thob pa yin no/_/sangs rgyas kyi sa kun tu 'od ni mtha' dang dbung med pa ma lus pa dri ma med pa'i chos thams cad la dbang sgyur ba ste/_kun tu shin tu lta ba/_sems can thams cad kyi don thams cad thob par byed pa'i mtshan nyid do/_/de la 
[\[47a.7\]]ting nge 'dzin rnams gang zhe na/_bcu po 'di dag ni byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi ting nge 'dzin rnams te/_bcu gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_rin chen kun tu 'phags pa dang /_shin tu gnas pa dang /_mi sgul ba dang /_phyir mi ldog pa dang /_rin chen 'byung gnas dang /_
[\[47b\]]
[\[47b.1\]]nyi ma'i 'od kyi gzi brjid dang /_don thams cad grub pa dang /_ye shes sgron ma dang /_da ltar gyi sangs rgyas mngon du bzhugs pa dang /_dpa' bar 'gro ba'i ting nge 'dzin te/_de dag la sogs pa byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi ting nge 'dzin ni mtha' yas so/_/gzungs ni 
[\[47b.2\]]bcu gnyis te/_bcu gnyis gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_dbang bskur ldan dang /_ye shes ldan dang /_sgra dbyangs rnam par dag pa dang /_mi zad pa'i za ma tog dang /_'khyil pa mtha' yas dang /_rgya mtsho'i phyag rgya dang /_pad ma bkod pa dang /_chags pa med pa'i sgor 'jug 
[\[47b.3\]]pa dang /_so so yang dag par rig pa rnam par gdon mi za ba la 'jug pa dang /_sangs rgyas kyi rgyan gyi byin gyis brlabs pa dang /_kha dog mtha' yas pa dang /_sangs rgyas kyi sku'i kha dog rdzogs pa mngon par bsgrub pa'i gzungs te/_de dag la sogs pa snga ma bzhin no/_/mngon par 
[\[47b.4\]]shes pa ni drug ste/_drug gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_lha'i mig dang /_lha'i rna ba dang /_pha rol gyi sems shes pa dang /_sngon gyi gnas rjes su dran pa'i shes pa dang /_rdzu 'phrul mngon par shes pa'i shes pa dang /_gang zag pa zad par shes pa'i mngon par shes pa ste drug po de dag go/_/
[\[47b.5\]]dbang ni bcu ste/_bcu gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_tshe'i tshad bskal pa grangs med dpag tu med par byin gyis rlob pas tshe la dbang ba dang /_ting nge 'dzin brjod du med pa'i yang brjod du med pa la nges par rtog cing 'jug pa'i tshul gyis sems la dbang ba dang /_'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du 
[\[47b.6\]]bkod pa'i rgyan du mas brgyan par byin gyis rlob pa ston pa'i tshul gyis yo byad la dbang ba dang /_dus ji bzhin du las kyi rnam par smin pa byin gyis rlob pa ston pas las la dbang ba dang /_'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du skye ba ston pas skye ba la dbang ba dang /_'jig rten gyi khams thams 
[\[47b.7\]]cad sangs rgyas kyis yongs su gang ba ston pas mos pa la dbang ba dang /_dus dang zhing gang du 'dod par mngon par rdzogs par byang chub pa ston pas smon lam la dbang ba dang /_'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du rdzu 'phrul gyis rnam par 'phrul pa mtha' yas pa ston pas rdzu 'phrul la dbang 
[\[48a\]]
[\[48a.1\]]ba dang /_chos kyi sgo snang ba mtha' dang dbung med pa yongs su ston pas chos la dbang ba dang /_de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs bcu dang /_mi 'jigs pa dang /_so so yang dag par rig pa dang /_ma 'dres pa dang /_mtshan dang /_dpe byad bzang po dang /_mngon 
[\[48a.2\]]par rdzogs pa'i byang chub dam pa dang /_dus thams cad du gtogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad kyi rdul shin tu phra ba'i grangs la sogs pa skad cig gcig la yongs su shes pa rnam pa thams cad kyi mchog dang ldan pa'i thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes mngon par rdzogs par byang 
[\[48a.3\]]chub pa ston pas ye shes la dbang ba ste/_bcu po de dag ni byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi dbang rnams so/_/de la byang chub sems dpa'i stobs bcu gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_bsam pa'i stobs dang /_lhag pa'i bsam pa'i stobs dang /_sbyor ba'i stobs dang /_shes rab 
[\[48a.4\]]kyi stobs dang /_smon lam gyi stobs dang /_spyod pa'i stobs dang /_theg pa'i stobs dang /_rnam par 'phrul ba'i stobs dang /_byang chub kyi stobs dang /_chos kyi 'khor lo rab tu skor ba'i stobs te/_bcu po de dag ni byang chub sems dpa'i stobs rnams so/_/de la byang 
[\[48a.5\]]chub sems dpa'i mi 'jigs pa bzhi gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_gzungs kyis thos pa 'dzin cing don bstan pa la mi 'jigs pa dang /_bdag med pa khong du chud pas gzhan gyis gtse ba'i mtshan ma mi 'byung zhing rang bzhin gyis spyod lam smad du med pa'i las gsum yongs su dag pa'i 
[\[48a.6\]]bsrung ba chen po phun sum tshogs pa'i mi 'jigs pa dang /_chos bzung ba yun du mi brjed pa dang /_thabs dang shes rab mthar phyin pas sems can sgrol zhing dad pa ston pa dang /_dge ba'i bar chad du mi 'gyur ba'i mi 'jigs pa dang /_thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi sems ma 
[\[48a.7\]]nyams shing theg pa gzhan gyis mi 'byung bar dbang yongs su rdzogs pa dang /_sems can gyi don rnam pa thams cad du yang dag par thob par bya ba la mi 'jigs pa ste/_bzhi po de dag ni byang chub sems dpa'i mi 'jigs pa rnams so/_/de la byang chub sems dpa'i chos ma 'dres pa 
[\[48b\]]
[\[48b.1\]{]bcwa [,]bco [}]brgyad gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_byang chub sems dpa' rnams ni ma bstan pa'i sbyin pa can rnams yin/_ma bstan pa'i tshul khrims can rnams dang /_ma bstan pa'i bzod pa can rnams dang /_ma bstan pa'i brtson 'grus can rnams dang /_ma bstan pa'i bsam gtan 
[\[48b.2\]]can rnams dang /_ma bstan pa'i shes rab can rnams dang /_bsdu ba'i dngos pos sems can thams cad sdud pa rnams dang /_yongs su bsngo ba'i cho ga shes pa rnams dang /_thabs la mkhas pas sems can thams cad kyi spyod pa'i dbang gi theg pa'i mchog gis 'byung ba ston pa 
[\[48b.3\]]rnams dang /_theg pa chen po las ma nyams pa rnams dang /_mya ngan las 'das pa'i sgo ston pa rnams dang /_zung dang snrel zhi'i rgyud la mkhas pa rnams dang /_ye shes sngon du 'gro ba'i mngon par 'du mi byed cing kha na ma tho ba med par tshe rabs thams cad du mngon par 'phags 
[\[48b.4\]]pa rnams dang /_lus dang ngag dang yid kyi las kyi mtha' dge ba bcu dang ldan pa rnams dang /_sdug bsngal gyi phung po thams cad bzod pa'i lus len pas sems can gyi khams thams cad yongs su mi gtong ba rnams dang /_'gro ba thams cad mngon par dga' bar ston pa rnams dang /_byis 
[\[48b.5\]]pa dang /_nyan thos mi bzad pa ji snyed cig gi nang na yang dge ba mang po'i rin po che'i shing dpag bsam ltar brtan pa'i thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi sems yongs su ma nyams pa rnams dang /_chos thams cad kyi thabs sbyin pas dbang bskur ba thob par bya ba'i phyir sangs rgyas kyi chos btsal 
[\[48b.6\]]ba bstan pa las mi ldog pa rnams te/[ {]bcwa [,]bco [}]brgyad po de dag ni byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi chos ma 'dres pa rnams so/_/de la de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs bcu gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_gnas dang gnas ma yin pa mkhyen pa'i stobs dang /_'das pa dang /_ma 'ongs pa dang /_
[\[48b.7\]]da ltar gyi las kyi rgyu'i rnam par smin pa len pa mkhyen pa'i stobs dang /_mos pa sna tshogs mkhyen pa'i stobs dang /_khams sna tshogs mkhyen pa'i stobs dang /_dbang po mchog dang mchog ma yin pa mkhyen pa'i stobs dang /_thams cad du 'gro ba'i lam mkhyen pa'i stobs dang /_
[\[49a\]]
[\[49a.1\]]bsam gtan dang /_rnam par thar pa dang /_ting nge 'dzin dang /_snyoms par 'jug pa dang /_kun nas nyon mongs pa dang /_rnam par byang ba dang /_rnam par ldang ba thams cad mkhyen pa'i stobs dang /_sngon gyi gnas rjes su dran pa mkhyen pa'i stobs dang /_
[\[49a.2\]]'chi 'pho dang skye ba mkhyen pa'i stobs dang /_zag pa zad par mkhyen pa'i stobs te/_bcu po de dag ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs rnams so/_/de la de bzhin gshegs pa'i mi 'jigs pa bzhi gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_chos thams cad mngon par rdzogs par byang chub pa la mi 
[\[49a.3\]]'jigs pa dang /_zag pa thams cad zad par shes pa la mi 'jigs pa dang /_bar du gcod pa'i chos rnams gzhan du mi 'gyur bar nges pa'i lung bstan pa la mi 'jigs pa dang /_phun sum tshogs pa thams cad thob par 'gyur ba'i 'byung ba'i lam de bzhin du 'gyur ba la mi 'jigs pa 
[\[49a.4\]]ste/_bzhi po de dag ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i mi 'jigs pa rnams so/_/de la sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa [{]bcwa [,]bco [}]brgyad gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_de bzhin gshegs pa la 'khrul ba med pa dang /_ca co med pa dang /_bsnyel ba med pa dang /_sems mnyam par ma bzhag pa 
[\[49a.5\]]med pa dang /_tha dad pa'i 'du shes med pa dang /_so sor ma brtags pa'i btang snyoms med pa dang /_mos pa nyams pa med pa dang /_brtson 'grus nyams pa med pa dang /_dran pa nyams pa med pa dang /_ting nge 'dzin nyams pa med pa dang /_shes rab nyams pa med pa dang /_
[\[49a.6\]]rnam par grol ba nyams pa med pa dang /_'das pa'i dus la ma chags ma thogs pa'i ye shes mthong ba 'jug pa dang /_ma 'ongs pa'i dus la ma chags ma thogs pa'i ye shes mthong ba 'jug pa dang /_da ltar gyi dus la ma chags ma thogs pa'i ye shes mthong ba 'jug pa dang /_
[\[49a.7\]]lus kyi las thams cad ye shes sngon du 'gro zhing ye shes kyi rjes su 'brang ba dang /_ngag gi las thams cad ye shes sngon du 'gro zhing ye shes kyi rjes su 'brang ba dang /_yid kyi las thams cad ye shes sngon du 'gro zhing ye shes kyi rjes su 'brang ba ste/[ {]bcwa [,]bco [}]brgyad po de dag ni sangs 
[\[49b\]]
[\[49b.1\]]rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa rnams so/_/de la de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying rje chen po gang zhe na/_rigs kyi bu de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying rje chen po ni rnam pa sum cu rtsa gnyis dang ldan par phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams mtha' yas mu med pa dag tu bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i rang 
[\[49b.2\]]bzhin du 'byung ste/_sum cu rtsa gnyis gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_chos thams cad ni bdag med pa na sems can rnams ni bdag med par ma mos pas de'i phyir sems can rnams la de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying rje chen po skye'o/_/chos thams cad ni sems can med pa na sems can 
[\[49b.3\]]rnams ni sems can du smra'o zhes sems can rnams la de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying rje chen po skye'o/_/chos thams cad ni srog med pa na sems can rnams ni srog tu smra'o zhes de'i phyir sems can rnams la de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying rje chen po skye'o/_/chos thams cad ni gang zag med pa 
[\[49b.4\]]na sems can rnams ni gang zag la gnas pa'o zhes sems can rnams la de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying rje chen po skye'o/_/chos thams cad ni med pa na sems can rnams ni yod par lta'o zhes sems can rnams la de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying rje chen po skye'o/_/chos thams cad ni gnas 
[\[49b.5\]]med pa na sems can rnams ni gnas la rten to zhes sems can rnams la de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying rje chen po skye'o/_/chos thams cad ni gzhi med pa na sems can rnams ni gzhi la dga'o zhes sems can rnams la de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying rje chen po skye'o/_/chos thams cad ni nga 
[\[49b.6\]]yir bya ba med pa na sems can rnams ni nga yir bya ba la rten to zhes sems can rnams la de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying rje chen po skye'o/_/chos thams cad ni bdag po med pa na sems can rnams ni yongs su bzung ba la brtson no zhes sems can rnams la de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying rje chen po skye'o/_/
[\[49b.7\]]chos thams cad ni dngos po med pa na sems can rnams ni dngos po la rten to zhes sems can rnams la de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying rje chen po skye'o/_/chos thams cad ma skyes pa na sems can rnams ni skye ba la rten to zhes sems can rnams la de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying rje chen po skye'o/_/
[\[50a\]]
[\[50a.1\]]chos thams cad ni ma 'phos ma byung ba na sems can rnams ni 'chi 'pho dang skye ba la rten to zhes sems can rnams la de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying rje chen po skye'o/_/chos thams cad ni kun nas nyon ma mongs pa na sems can rnams ni kun nas nyon mongs so zhes sems can rnams la 
[\[50a.2\]]de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying rje chen po skye'o/_/chos thams cad ni 'dod chags dang bral ba na sems can rnams ni 'dod chags par byed do zhes de'i phyir sems can rnams la de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying rje chen po skye'o/_/chos thams cad ni zhe sdang dang bral ba na sems can rnams zhe sdang 
[\[50a.3\]]bar byed do zhes de'i phyir sems can rnams la de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying rje chen po skye'o/_/chos thams cad ni gti mug dang bral ba na sems can rnams ni gti mug par gyur to zhes de'i phyir sems can rnams la de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying rje chen po skye'o/_/chos thams cad ni 'ong 
[\[50a.4\]]ba med pa na sems can rnams ni 'ong ba la gnas pa'o zhes de'i phyir sems can rnams la de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying rje chen po skye'o/_/chos thams cad ni 'gro ba med pa na sems can rnams ni 'gro ba la gnas so zhes de'i phyir sems can rnams la de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying rje chen po skye'o/_/
[\[50a.5\]]chos thams cad ni mngon par 'du byed pa med pa na sems can rnams ni mngon par 'du byed pa la spyod do zhes de'i phyir sems can rnams la de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying rje chen po skye'o/_/chos thams cad ni spros pa med pa na sems can rnams ni spros pa la dga'o zhes de'i phyir sems 
[\[50a.6\]]can rnams la de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying rje chen po skye'o/_/chos thams cad ni stong pa na sems can rnams ni lta ba la zhen to zhes de'i phyir sems can rnams la de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying rje chen po skye'o/_/chos thams cad ni mtshan ma med pa na sems can rnams ni mtshan ma'i 
[\[50a.7\]]spyod yul can no zhes de'i phyir sems can rnams la de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying rje chen po skye'o/_/chos thams cad ni smon pa med pa na sems can rnams ni smon lam la spyod do zhes de'i phyir sems can rnams la de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying rje chen po skye'o/_/kye ma 'jig rten 
[\[50b\]]
[\[50b.1\]]gnas pa 'di ni phan tshun rtsod pas yongs su zin cing gnod sems kyi tha ba'i skyon la zhugs par gzigs nas/_de dag la gnod sems kyi tha ba'i skyon spong ba'i phyir chos bstan par bya'o zhes sems can rnams la de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying rje chen po skye'o/_/kye ma 'jig rten 
[\[50b.2\]]gnas pa 'di ni phyin ci log dang ldan zhing lam nyam nga bar zhugs pa lam gol ba la gnas pa yin gyis/_de dag la yang dag pa'i lam du gzud par bya'o zhes sems can rnams la de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying rje chen po skye'o/_/kye ma 'jig rten gnas pa 'di ni brkam zhing 'dod 
[\[50b.3\]]pa'i zil gyis non pa ste/_chog mi shes shing gzhan gyi nor la 'phrog pa yin gyis de dag 'phags pa'i nor dad pa dang /_tshul khrims dang /_thos pa dang /_gtong ba dang /_shes rab dang /_ngo tsha shes pa dang khrel yod pa'i nor la dgod do zhes sems can rnams la de bzhin 
[\[50b.4\]]gshegs pa'i snying rje chen po skye'o/_/kye ma sems can 'di dag ni bu smad dang /_khyim dang /_nor dang /_'bru la sred pas 'khol ba ste/_snying po med pa la snying por 'du shes pa yin gyis de dag la shin tu mi rtag par bstan par bya'o zhes de'i phyir sems can rnams la de bzhin 
[\[50b.5\]]gshegs pa'i snying rje chen po skye'o/_/kye ma sems can 'di dag ni mi rigs pas 'tsho ba ste/_gcig la gcig 'drid par gnas pa yin gyis de dag la 'tsho ba yongs su dag par bya ba'i phyir chos bstan to zhes sems can rnams la de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying rje chen po skye'o/_/
[\[50b.6\]]kye ma sems can 'di dag ni chog mi shes pa ste/_rnyed pa dang /_bkur sti dang /_tshigs su bcad pa la spyod kyang bdag cag ni chog shes pa'o snyam du 'dzin gyis/_de dag la gang 'di sdug bsngal shin tu zhi zhing bde ba'i mya ngan las 'das pas shin tu chog par bya'o zhes sems can rnams la 
[\[50b.7\]]de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying rje chen po skye'o/_/kye ma sems can 'di dag ni khyim gyi gnas sdug bsngal gyi snod shin tu nyon mongs pa la rtag tu dga' ba yin gyis de dag las byung zhing khams gsum pa las yang dag par 'da' bar bya ba'i phyir chos bstan to zhes sems can rnams la de bzhin 
[\[51a\]]
[\[51a.1\]]gshegs pa'i snying rje chen po skye'o/_/chos thams cad ni rgyus nye bar 'gro ba ste/_bsgrubs pas nye bar gnas pa'i mtshan nyid yin na/_de la sems can rnams ni 'phags pa'i rnam par grol ba la le lo byed kyis de dag la rnam par grol ba lhur blang ba'i phyir 
[\[51a.2\]]brtson 'grus brtsam par chos bstan to zhes sems can rnams la de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying rje chen po skye'o/_/sems can rnams ni chags pa med pa'i ye shes dam pa khyad par du 'phags pa'i yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa 'di btang zhing gang 'di nyan thos kyi theg pa dang /_rang sangs rgyas 
[\[51a.3\]]kyi theg pa dman pa tshol ba yin gyis de dag la gang 'di sangs rgyas kyi ye shes la dmigs par bya ba'i phyir rgya chen po la blo mos par bya'o zhes sems can rnams la de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying rje chen po skye ste/_rigs kyi bu rnam pa sum cu rtsa gnyis po de dag dang ldan na sems can rnams 
[\[51a.4\]]la de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying rje chen po skye'o/_/de bas na de bzhin gshegs pa thugs rje chen po la gnas pa zhes bya ste/_rigs kyi bu byang chub sems dpa' gang la la zhig sems can dag la rnam pa sum cu rtsa gnyis po de dag dang ldan pa'i snying rje chen po skye na byang chub sems dpa' 
[\[51a.5\]]sems dpa' chen po de ni bsod nams kyi zhing gzi brjid chen pos rgyas pa phyir mi ldog pa sems can gyi don la brtson par rig par bya'o/_/snying rje chen po'i chos kyi sgo'i le'u 'di bcom ldan 'das kyis bshad pa na srog chags stong phrag sum cu rtsa gnyis ni bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs 
[\[51a.6\]]pa'i byang chub tu sems skyes so/_/srog chags brgya stong ni mi skye ba'i chos la bzod pa thob bo/_/lha'i bu stong phrag bdun cu rtsa gnyis ni chos rnams la chos kyi mig rdul med cing dri ma dang bral ba rnam par dag go/_/rigs kyi bu de ltar de dag la sogs pa de bzhin gshegs pa 
[\[51a.7\]]rnams dang /_byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi dbang sgyur gyi chos kyi rnam par 'god pa ni dpag tu med grangs med de/_de dag phyi ma'i mtha'i mur thug pa'i bskal par de bzhin gshegs pas bstan kyang mtha' med na sems can rnams kyi dga' ba dang /_dad pa dang /_rangs pa 
[\[51b\]]
[\[51b.1\]]bskyed pa'i phyir phyogs tsam zhig bstan te/_de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' sems can thams cad la chos dkar po las 'thun par 'byung ba'i dbugs 'byung ba dang /_rngub pa'i srog nye bar sbyor ba la mkhas pa yin no/_/ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' chos kyi char sprin dpag tu 
[\[51b.2\]]med pa kun 'dzin nus pa yin zhe na/_rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_dper na rlung mar ni rlung gi dkyil 'khor mu med cing rgya che la brtan zhing sra bas 'jig rten gyi khams 'jig pa dang 'byung ba char sprin dang bcas pa/_rgya mtsho dang /_gling chen po bzhi par bcas pa/_ri rab dang /_ri

[\[51b.4\]]sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po yang gzungs kyi rlung mar gyi dkyil 'khor mu med pas yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi sprin gyis phab pa'i gtan pa med pa'i chos kyi char gyi rgyun chen po thams cad yongs su 'dzin to/_/'jig rten gyi khams thams cad 'byung ba'i tshe ji skad du brjod pa'i ri rab 
[\[51b.5\]]dang /_rgya mtsho dang /_gling chen po bzhi pa dang /_ri btang bzung dang /_ri btang bzung chen po dang /_gangs dang /_khor yug ri dang /_khor yug ri chen po dang /_ri spos kyi ngad ldang ba dang /_grong rdal dang /_'bab chu dang /_nags tshal dang /_gzhal med khang gi rnam pa lta bu

[\[52a\]]
[\[52a.1\]]dpag tu med pa kun 'dzin nus pa yin no/_/ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' chos rnam pa tha dad pa'i gzhal med khang chen po'i bkod pa rnam par dbye ba thams cad rnam par 'byed cing yongs su 'god pa yin zhe na/_rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_dper na rlung mar ni

[\[56b.3\]]sems mod kyi/_'on kyang chos nyid thob pas 'od med par 'gyur ro/_/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' nyi ma shar na yang mu stegs can ngan pa'i 'od dang /_mu stegs can ngan pa'i lugs thams cad 'od med par 'gyur mod kyi/_byang chub sems dpa'i nyi ma ni ngas 'di dag 'od med 
[\[56b.4\]]par bya'o snyam du mi sems mod kyi/_'on kyang chos nyid thob pas 'od med par 'gyur ro/_/rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_dper na nyi ma shar na 'dzam bu'i gling gi mi rnams la mnyam pa dang mi mnyam pa legs par ston to/_/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' nyi ma shar na yang sems can 
[\[56b.5\]]thams cad la ye shes kyi snang bas mnyam pa dang /_mi mnyam pa legs par ston to/_/rigs kyi bu mnyam pa zhes bya ba 'di ni 'phags pa'i lam gyi tshig [{]bla dgas [,]bla dwags [}]so/_/rigs kyi bu mi mnyam pa zhes bya ba 'di ni lam ngan pa'i tshig [{]bla dgas [,]bla dwags [}]so/_/rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_dper na nyi ma

[\[61a\]]
[\[61a.1\]]te/_de ltar na de bzhin gshegs pa'i bstan pa la skur pa ma btab pa yin no/_/rigs kyi bu chos bcu po de dag dang ldan na byang chub sems dpa' sems rnam par dag pa rnams yin no/_/rigs kyi bu chos bcu dang ldan na byang chub sems dpa' sems nem nur 
[\[61a.2\]]med pa rnams yin no/_/bcu gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku'i gsang ba la mngon par dad/_de bzhin gshegs pa'i gsung gi gsang ba dang /_de bzhin gshegs pa'i yid kyi gsang ba dang /_byang chub sems dpa' yang dag par sgrub pa dang /_
[\[61a.3\]]byang chub dang /_de bzhin gshegs pa 'byung ba dang /_theg pa gcig gi tshul bstan pa dang /_tshul sna tshogs bstan pa dang /_de bzhin gshegs pa'i gsung gi sgra dbyangs zab mo dang /_sems can gyi bsam pa ji lta ba bzhin du 'dul ba la mngon par dad do/_/rigs kyi 
[\[61a.4\]]bu de la ji ltar na de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku'i gsang ba la mngon par dad pa nas ji ltar na sems can gyi bsam pa ji lta ba bzhin du 'dul ba la mngon par dad pa'i bar du yin zhe na/_rigs kyi bu 'di la byang chub sems dpa' 'di snyam du sems so/_/gang 'di de bzhin gshegs 
[\[61a.5\]]pa'i chos kyi sku dang /_zhi ba'i sku dang /_mi mnyam pa'i sku dang /_mi mnyam pa dang mnyam pa'i sku dang /_tshad med pa'i sku dang /_phal la med pa'i sku dang /_rdo rje'i sku zhes bya ba thos pa 'di ni yang dag pa'o/_/'di ni de kho na'o/_/'di ni mi slu ba'o snyam ste/_gnas 'di la nem 
[\[61a.6\]]nur med cing the tsom med de de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku'i gsang ba la mngon par dad do/_/ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs pa'i gsung gi gsang ba la mngon par dad ce na/_de 'di ltar sems so/_/gang 'di de bzhin gshegs pa'i gsung 
[\[61a.7\]]gi gsang ba de bzhin gshegs pa ni mngon sum du lung ston pa dang /_gsang ste lung ston pa dang /_ldem po ngag gis kyang sems can rnams la lung ston mod kyi/_de bzhin gshegs pa la 'khrul pa'am ca co med de/_de'i phyir de la [{]brdzun [,]rdzun [}]du smra ba'i nyes pa mi 'byung ngo /_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_
[\[61b\]]
[\[61b.1\]]de bzhin gshegs pa ni skyon thams cad spangs pa/_dri ma thams cad spangs pa/_rdul thams cad spangs pa/_yongs su gdung ba thams cad dang bral ba/_nyon mongs pa med pa/_dbang du gyur pa/_zhi ba/_dang ba/_yongs su dag pa/_rnyog pa med pa/_rnam par gsal ba ste/_
[\[61b.2\]]gang de bzhin gshegs pa rnams la 'khrul pa'am/_ca co 'byung ba de ni gnas ma yin zhing skabs med do zhes thos pa 'di ni yang dag pa'o/_/'di ni de kho na'o/_/'di ni mi slu ba'o snyam ste/_gnas 'di la nem nur med/_the tsom med de de ltar na de bzhin gshegs pa'i gsung gi 
[\[61b.3\]]gsang ba la mngon par dad do/_/ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs pa'i yid kyi gsang ba la mngon par dad ce na/_de 'di ltar sems so/_/gang 'di de bzhin gshegs pa'i yid kyi gsang ba de bzhin gshegs pas ci dgongs pa'i don gang sems dang 'grogs pa/_
[\[61b.4\]]sems la gnas pa de ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i byin gyi rlabs kyis gyur pa ma gtogs par nyan thos dang /_rang sangs rgyas thams cad dang /_byang chub sems dpa' thams cad dang /_sems can thams cad kyis shes par mi nus so/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_rigs kyi bu de 
[\[61b.5\]]bzhin gshegs pa ni zab cing gting dpag dka' ba/_brtag par bya ba ma yin pa/_rtog ge'i spyod yul las shin tu 'das pa/_dpag tu med pa/_rgya che ba/_yangs pa/_nam mkha'i dbyings dang mtshungs pa/_rtog ge thams cad kyi yul las 'das par thos pa 'di ni 
[\[61b.6\]]yang dag pa'o/_'di ni de kho na'o/_/'di ni mi slu ba'o snyam ste/_gnas 'di la nem nur med the tsom med pa yin te/_de ltar na de bzhin gshegs pa'i yid kyi gsang ba la mngon par dad do/_/ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' byang chub sems dpa'i yang dag par bsgrub pa la 
[\[61b.7\]]mngon par dad ce na/_de 'di ltar sems so/_/gang 'di lta ste/_byang chub kyi rgyu/_byang chub kyi gzhi/_byang chub kyi phyir byang chub sems dpa' de dag sems can gyi don la mngon du phyogs pa dang /_sems can gyi bya ba dang /_sems can gyi byed pa dang /_yongs su mi skyo 
[\[62a\]]
[\[62a.1\]]ba dang /_mi skrag pa dang /_khur chen po khyer ba dang /_mthu brtan pa dang /_rtul ba brtan pa dang /_pha rol tu phyin pa yang dag par sgrub pa dang /_sangs rgyas kyi chos rnams mthar gyis yang dag par sgrub pa dang /_thogs pa med pa'i blo dang /_mtha' 
[\[62a.2\]]yas pa'i blo dang /_mi mnyam pa'i blo dang /_phal la med pa'i blo dang /_brtson 'grus brtan pa dang /_go cha brtan pa dang /_dam bcas pa brtan pa dang /_dam bcas pa las mi g.yo ba dang /_dam bcas pa phal la med pa de dag thams cad rim gyis 'phel zhing rgyas 
[\[62a.3\]]pa dang /_yangs par yongs su rdzogs par 'gyur ro zhes thos pa 'di ni yang dag pa'o/_/'di ni de kho na'o/_/'di ni mi slu ba'o snyam ste/_gnas 'di la de nem nur med/_the tsom med de de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' yang dag par sgrub pa la mngon par dad do/_/ji ltar na 
[\[62a.4\]]byang chub sems dpa' byang chub dang de bzhin gshegs pa 'byung ba la mngon par dad ce na/_de 'di ltar sems so/_/gang byang chub sems dpa' byang chub kyi snying por 'dug nas lha'i mig chags pa med cing thogs pa med pa sgrib pa med pa 'thob bo/_/lha'i rna ba dang /_gzhan gyi sems 
[\[62a.5\]]shes pa dang /_sngon gyi gnas rjes su dran pa'i shes pa dang /_rdzu 'phrul gyi rnam pa shes pa dang /_zag pa zad pa shes pa dang /_dus gsum pa skad cig gcig dang ldan pas shes pa chags pa med pa/_thogs pa med pa/_sgrib pa med pa 'thob po/_/de'i phyir sems can gyi 
[\[62a.6\]]khams la yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhin du lta ste/_sems can 'di rnams ni lus kyis ngan du spyod pa dang ldan pa/_ngag gis ngan du spyod pa dang ldan pa/_yid kyis ngan du spyod pa dang ldan pa/_'phags pa la skur pa 'debs pa/_log par lta ba rnams te/_log par lta ba'i las 
[\[62a.7\]]dang /_chos yang dag par blangs pa'i phyir de'i rgyu de'i rkyen de'i gzhis lus zhig nas shi ba'i 'og tu ngan 'gro log par ltung ba sems can dmyal ba dag tu skye'o/_/sems can 'di rnams ni lus kyis legs par spyod pa dang ldan pa/_ngag gis legs par spyod pa dang ldan pa/_yid 
[\[62b\]]
[\[62b.1\]]kyis legs par spyod pa dang ldan pa/_'phags pa la skur pa mi 'debs pa/_yang dag par lta ba rnams te/_yang dag par lta ba'i las dang /_chos yang dag par blangs pa'i phyir de'i rgyu de'i rkyen de'i gzhis lus zhig nas bde 'gro mtho ris kyi 'jig rten du lha'i nang du skye'o snyam nas de ltar 
[\[62b.2\]]yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhin du sems can gyi khams la lta ste/_sems can gyi khams de nyid kyi dge ba dang /_mi dge ba'i las rnams la rnam par lta'o/_/de yang 'di snyam du sems te/_sngon bdag byang chub sems dpa'i spyad pa spyod pas gang 'di ltar bdag nyid rnam par sangs rgyas 
[\[62b.3\]]nas gzhan yang rnam par sangs rgyas par bya'o zhes dam bcas pa'i bdag gi smon lam de yongs su rdzogs so/_/bdag gi brnag pa de yongs su rdzogs so snyam mo zhes thos pa 'di ni yang dag pa'o/_/'di ni de kho na'o/_/'di ni mi slu ba'o snyam ste/_gnas 'di la nem nur med/_
[\[62b.4\]]the tsom med do/_/gang la byang chub sems dpa' de rnam par sangs rgyas pa de ni byang chub ces bya'o/_/bdag nyid rnam par sangs rgyas pas na rdzogs par sangs rgyas zhes bya ste/_de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' byang chub dang /_de bzhin gshegs pa 'byung ba la mngon par dad do/_/ji 
[\[62b.5\]]ltar na byang chub sems dpa' theg pa gcig gi tshul bstan pa la mngon par dad ce na/_de 'di ltar sems so/_/gang 'di theg pa ni gcig kho na te/_'di lta ste/_de bzhin gshegs pa'i theg pa'o zhes thos pa 'di ni yang dag pa'o/_/'di ni de kho na'o/_/'di ni mi slu ba'o/_/
[\[62b.6\]]'di ni de bzhin pa'o/_/ma nor pa'o/_/gzhan ma yin pa'o/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_theg pa gzhan rnams kyang 'di las byung ngo /_/rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_dper na 'dzam bu'i gling na gang gling yod pa rnams ming gis tha dad par shes mod kyi/_'on kyang de dag ni 'dzam bu'i 
[\[62b.7\]]gling du 'du'o/_/'dzam bu'i gling la rten to/_/'dzam bu'i gling gi grangs su 'gro'o/_/de'i phyir na gling gcig kho na ste/_de ni 'dzam bu'i gling ngo /_/de bzhin du theg pa thams cad ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i theg par 'du'o/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i theg pa la gnas so/_/
[\[63a\]]
[\[63a.1\]]de bzhin gshegs pa'i theg pa las byung ba'o/_/de'i phyir theg pa gcig kho na ste/_'di ltar de bzhin gshegs pa'i theg pa'o snyam ste/_gnas 'di la de nem nur med/_the tsom med de de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' theg pa gcig gi tshul bstan pa la mngon 
[\[63a.2\]]par dad do/_/ji ltar na tshul sna tshogs bstan pa la mngon par dad ce na/_de 'di ltar sems so/_/gang 'di mdo sde de dang de dag las de bzhin gshegs pa'i tshul sna tshogs bshad pa thos pa 'di ni yang dag pa'o/_/'di ni de kho na'o/_/'di ni mi slu ba'o/_/de ci'i phyir

[\[63a.4\]]dpa' de bzhin gshegs pa'i gsung sgra'i dbyangs zab mo la mngon par dad ce na/_de 'di ltar sems so/_/gang 'di de bzhin gshegs pa'i gsung sgra'i dbyangs zab mo thos pa 'di ni yang dag pa'o/_/'di ni de kho na'o/_/'di ni mi slu ba'o/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_
[\[63a.5\]]dge ba'i rtsa ba chung ngu'i lha'i bu rnams kyi dbyangs kyang zab cing snyan pa yin na/_bskal pa brgya stong du mar dge ba'i rtsa ba bsgrubs pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi lta ci smos snyam ste/_gnas 'di la de nem nur med/_the tsom med de/_de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' de bzhin 
[\[63a.6\]]gshegs pa'i gsung sgra'i dbyangs zab mo la mngon par dad do/_/rigs kyi bu ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' sems can gyi bsam pa ji lta ba bzhin du 'dul ba la mngon par dad ce na/_de 'di ltar sems so/_/gang 'di de bzhin gshegs pa bsam pa dang /_bag la nyal mkhyen te/_sems 
[\[63a.7\]]can mos pa tha dad pa dang /_'dul ba tha dad pa rnams la gsung sgra'i dbyangs gcig brjod pas the tsom gcod cing yongs su smin pa ji lta ba bzhin du sems can rnams 'dul lo/_/sems can rnams kyang so so nas de bzhin gshegs pa bdag la chos ston te/_/de bzhin gshegs pa bdag la 
[\[63b\]]
[\[63b.1\]]chos ston to snyam byed kyang de bzhin gshegs pa ni mi rtog rnam par mi rtog go zhes thos pa 'di ni yang dag pa'o/_/'di ni de kho na'o/_/'di ni mi slu ba'o snyam ste/_gnas 'di la de nem nur med/_the tsom med de/_rigs kyi bu de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' sems can gyi bsam pa 
[\[63b.2\]]ji lta ba bzhin du 'dul ba la mngon par dad do/_/rigs kyi bu chos bcu po de dag dang ldan na byang chub sems dpa' sems nem nur med pa rnams yin no/_/rigs kyi bu chos bcu dang ldan na byang chub sems dpa' blo rgya mtsho dang mtshungs pa rnams yin no/_/bcu gang zhe na/_'di lta 
[\[63b.3\]]ste/_rin po che'i 'byung gnas su gyur pa rnams yin/_zab cing gting dpag dka' ba rnams yin/_rgya che zhing tshad med pa rnams yin/_bags kyis gzhol ba rnams yin/_nyon mongs pa'i ro dang lhan cig mi 'dug pa rnams yin/_ro gcig pa rnams yin/_chu bo kun 
[\[63b.4\]]zed pa rnams yin/_dus las mi yol ba rnams yin/_'byung po mang po'i gnas su gyur pa rnams yin/_mi zad pa rnams yin no/_/rigs kyi bu de la ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' rin po che'i 'byung gnas su gyur pa yin pa nas ji ltar na mi zad pa'i bar du yin zhe na/_rigs kyi 
[\[63b.5\]]bu 'di lta ste/_dper na rgya mtsho chen po ni rin po che thams cad kyi 'byung gnas yin te/_'dzam bu'i gling gi mi thams cad kyis rin po che blang bas mi zad do/_/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang yon tan rin po che thams cad kyi 'byung gnas yin te/_sems can thams cad kyis yon

[\[63b.7\]]bu 'di lta ste/_dper na rgya mtsho chen po ni 'di lta ste/_chu tshad med pas na yangs shing rgya che ba yin no/_/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang 'di lta ste/_blo tshad med pas na yangs shing rgya che ba yin no/_/rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_dper na rgya mtsho chen po ni bags kyis gzhol/_

[\[65b\]]
[\[65b.1\]]de yang de dag la spobs pas na de'i phyir rigs par spobs pa rnams zhes bya'o/_/rigs kyi bu chos bcu po de dag dang ldan na byang chub sems dpa' rigs pa'i spobs pa can rnams yin no/_/rigs kyi bu chos bcu dang ldan na byang chub sems dpa' grol ba'i spobs pa can rnams yin no/_/
