[\[79a\]]
[\[79a.1\]]dpa'i lus nor bu rin po che ni de tsam du khyab par byed pa ma yin no/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_de ni 'di ltar dri ma'i lhag ma las pa'i phyir ro/_/rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_dper na tshes gnyis kyi zla ba ni zla ba zhes bya ba'i grangs su 'gro ba nas/_zla ba nya ba'i bar gyi zla ba yang zla ba zhes 
[\[79a.2\]]bya ba'i grangs su 'gro mod kyi/_tshes gnyis kyi zla ba ci 'dra ba de lta bur zla ba nya ba ma yin no/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_de ni 'di ltar chos nyid thob pa'i phyir te/_rigs kyi bu de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa'i lus dang /_de bzhin gshegs pa'i skur bcas te lus kyi grangs su 'gro mod kyi/_byang chub 
[\[79a.3\]]sems dpa'i lus ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku'i drung na lam mer mi 'gyur/_lhan ner mi 'gyur/_lhang nger mi 'gyur ro/_/rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste dper na/_zla ba nya ba'i drung na tshes gnyis kyi zla ba ji lta ba de bzhin du de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku dang /_byang chub sems dpa'i lus kyang lus su brjod pa'i 
[\[79a.4\]]mtshan nyid kyis mtshan nyid gcig mod kyi/_yon tan gyi mthu'i mtshan nyid kyis ni ma yin no/_/rigs kyi bu chos bcu dang ldan na byang chub sems dpa' lus rdo rje ltar sra ba rnams yin no/_/bcu gang zhe na/_'di lta ste 'dod chags dang /_zhe sdang dang /_gti mug dag gis mi tshugs pa rnams yin/_khro 
[\[79a.5\]]ba dang /_gnod sems kyi tha ba dang /_dri ma dang /_nga rgyal dang /_rgyags pa dang /_dregs pa dang /_lta ba phyin ci log dag gis mi tshugs pa rnams yin/_'jig rten gyi chos brgyad po de dag gis mi tshugs pa rnams yin/_ngan song gi sdug bsngal thams cad kyis mi tshugs pa rnams yin/_sems 
[\[79a.6\]]can gyi sdug bsngal dag gis mi tshugs pa rnams yin/_skye ba dang /_rga ba dang /_na ba dang /_'chi ba'i sdug bsngal dag gis mi tshugs pa rnams yin/_mu stegs can dang pha rol rgol ba thams cad kyis mi tshugs pa rnams yin/_bdud thams cad dang bdud kyi ris su gtogs pa'i lha thams cad kyis 
[\[79a.7\]]mi tshugs pa rnams yin/_nyan thos dang /_rang sangs rgyas thams cad kyis mi tshugs pa rnams yin/_'dod pa la spyod pa thams cad kyis mi tshugs pa rnams yin te/_rigs kyi bu chos bcu po de dag dang ldan na byang chub sems dpa' lus rdo rje ltar sra ba rnams yin no/_/rigs kyi bu chos bcu dang ldan 
[\[79b\]]
[\[79b.1\]]na byang chub sems dpa' ded dpon chen po rnams yin te/_bcu gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_bkur ba rnams yin/_mchod par 'os pa rnams yin/_phyin par byed pa rnams yin/_brtan par bya ba rnams yin/_nye bar 'tsho bar bya ba rnams yin/_rgyags shin tu phun sum tshogs pa rnams yin/_
[\[79b.2\]]tshogs shin tu bsags pa rnams yin/_mi ngoms pa rnams yin/_thog mar 'gro ba rnams yin/_thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi grong khyer chen por phyin par bya ba la mkhas pa rnams yin no/_/rigs kyi bu de la ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' bkur ba yin pa nas ji ltar na thams cad mkhyen 
[\[79b.3\]]pa'i grong khyer chen por phyin par bya ba la mkhas pa'i bar du yin zhe na/_rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_dper na ded dpon chen po ni rgyal po dang /_rgyal po chen po'i blon po dag gis bkur ba yin no/_/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' ded dpon chen po yang sangs rgyas rnams dang /_sangs rgyas 
[\[79b.4\]]kyi nyan thos rnams kyis bkur ba yin no/_/rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste dper na/_ded dpon chen po ni bram ze dang /_rgyal rigs dang /_grong ba dang /_yul gyi mi rnams kyis mchod par 'os pa rnams yin no/_/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' ded dpon chen po yang slob pa dang /_mi slob pa dang /_
[\[79b.5\]]gzhan yang lha dang /_klu dang /_gnod sbyin dang /_dri za dang /_lha ma yin dang /_nam mkha' lding dang /_mi 'am ci dang /_lto 'phye chen po dag gis mchod par 'os pa yin no/_/rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_dper na ded dpon chen po ni 'brog dgon par lam grogs kyi tshogs 'chan pas gtses 
[\[79b.6\]]pa dag legs par bde zhing yongs su mi skyo bar phyin par byed pa yin te/_de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' ded dpon chen po yang 'khor ba'i 'brog dgon pa chen po'i nang na sems can lam grogs kyi tshogs nyon mongs pa'i 'chan pas gtses pa dag legs par bde zhing yongs su mi skyo bar phyin par byed pa 
[\[79b.7\]]yin no/_/rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_dper na ded dpon chen po la brten na/_sdug bsngal ba dang /_phongs pa dag 'brog dgon pa nas 'byung bar 'gyur ro/_/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' ded dpon chen po la brten na mu stegs can gzhan spyod pa can dang /_kun tu rgyu dang /_gcer bu ba rnams 
[\[80a\]]
[\[80a.1\]]'khor ba'i 'brog dgon pa chen po nas 'byung bar 'gyur ro/_/rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_dper na ded dpon chen po ni rgyal po dang /_las byed pa'i mi dang /_zho shas 'tsho ba dang /_de ma yin pa'i sems can gzhan rnams nye bar 'tsho ba yin te/_de bzhin du byang chub sems 
[\[80a.2\]]dpa'i ded dpon chen po yang 'khor ba la mngon par dga' ba'i sems can rnams nye bar 'tsho ba yin no/_/rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_dper na ded dpon chen po ni nam phyogs su 'gro bar chas te lam grogs mang po dang lhan cig tu grong khyer chen por phyin par bya ba'i phyir 'brog dgon par zhugs pa de'i tshe/_
[\[80a.3\]]grong khyer chen por phyin gyi bar du rgyags phun sum tshogs pas 'brog dgon pa de nas legs par bde zhing yongs su skyo ba med par 'byung bar byed do/_/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' ded dpon chen po yang nam sangs rgyas kyis bsten pa'i phyogs su 'gro bar 'dod cing sems can lam grogs mang po dang de 
[\[80a.4\]]'khor ba'i 'brog dgon pa chen por 'jug par 'dod la thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi grong khyer chen por phyin par bya bar 'dod pa de'i tshe bsod nams kyi rgyags kyi tshogs shin tu phun sum tshogs pas 'gro'o/_/rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_dper na ded dpon chen po ni 'tsho ba'i yo byad 'di lta ste/_gser dang /_
[\[80a.5\]]nor bu dang /_mu tig dang /_bai dUr+ya dang /_dung dang /_man shel dang /_byi ru la sogs pa mang po bsags shing phyogs su 'gro'o/_/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' ded dpon chen po yang nam thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi grong khyer chen por mngon par zhugs pa de'i tshe sangs rgyas kyi chos kyi tshogs 
[\[80a.6\]]shin tu bsags shing 'gro'o/_/rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_dper na ded dpon chen po ni nor bsags pas chog mi shes pa yin no/_/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' ded dpon chen po yang chos kyi nor bsags pas chog mi shes pa yin no/_/rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_dper na ded dpon chen po ni 'di lta 
[\[80a.7\]]ste/_nor phun sum tshogs pa dang /_gtso bo dang /_dbang yod pa dang /_ngag mnyan pas lam grogs kyi tshogs thams cad kyi thog mar 'gro ba yin no/_/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' ded dpon chen po yang 'di lta ste/_yon tan phun sum tshogs pa dang /_yongs kyi gtso bo dang /_chos 
[\[80b\]]
[\[80b.1\]]la dbang ba dang /_ma log par smra bas sems can lam grogs kyi tshogs thams cad kyi thog mar 'gro ba yin no/_/rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_dper na ded dpon chen po ni grong khyer chen por phyin par bya ba la mkhas pa yin no/_/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' ded dpon chen po yang thams cad 
[\[80b.2\]]mkhyen pa nyid kyi grong khyer chen por phyin par bya ba la mkhas pa yin te/_rigs kyi bu de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' bkur ba yin pas na thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi grong khyer chen por phyin par bya ba la mkhas pa'i bar du yin no/_/rigs kyi bu chos bcu po de dag dang ldan na byang chub sems dpa' ded 
[\[80b.3\]]dpon chen po rnams yin no/_/rigs kyi bu chos bcu dang ldan na byang chub sems dpa' lam la mkhas pa rnams yin no/_/bcu gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_lam mnyam pa yang rab tu shes/_lam nyam nga ba'ang rab tu shes/_lam bde ba'ang rab tu shes/_'tsham pa'i lam yang rab 
[\[80b.4\]]tu shes/_lam chu che ba dang /_chu med pa yang rab tu shes/_lam gyi dgongs mal dag kyang rab tu shes/_lam gyis tshugs dag kyang rab tu shes/_lam 'khyog pa dag kyang rab tu shes/_lam drang po dag kyang rab tu shes/_lam 'byung ba yang rab tu shes so/_/rigs kyi bu chos 
[\[80b.5\]]bcu po de dag dang ldan na byang chub sems dpa' lam la mkhas pa rnams yin no/_/rigs kyi bu chos bcu dang ldan na byang chub sems dpa' lam ma log par ston pa rnams yin no/_/bcu gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_theg pa chen pos 'dul ba'i sems can rnams la/_byang chub sems 
[\[80b.6\]]dpa'i lam ston gyi nyan thos kyi lam mi ston to/_/nyan thos kyis 'dul ba'i sems can rnams la nyan thos kyi lam ston gyi byang chub sems dpa'i lam mi ston to/_/thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyis 'dul ba'i sems can rnams la thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi lam ston gyi/_rang sangs rgyas kyi lam 
[\[80b.7\]]mi ston to/_/rang sangs rgyas kyis 'dul ba'i sems can rnams la rang sangs rgyas kyi lam ston gyi thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi lam mi ston to/_/bdag tu 'dzin pa la mngon par zhen pa'i sems can rnams dang /_chos su 'dzin pa la mngon par zhen pa rnams la bdag med pa'i lam dang /_stong pa 
[\[81a\]]
[\[81a.1\]]nyid kyi lam ston gyi/_bdag dang sems can dang /_srog dang /_gso ba dang /_skyes bu dang /_gang zag gi lam mi ston to/_/mtha' gnyis la gnas pa'i sems can rnams la mtha' gnyis dang bral ba'i lam ston gyi/_mtha' gnyis la gnas pa mi ston to/_/
[\[81a.2\]]sems g.yengs pa'i sems can rnams la zhi gnas dang /_lhag mthong gi lam ston gyi g.yeng ba'i lam mi ston to/_/spros pa la mngon par dga' ba'i sems can rnams la de bzhin nyid kyi lam ston gyi byis pa spros pa la mngon par dga' ba'i lam mi ston to/_/'khor ba la mngon par dga' 
[\[81a.3\]]ba'i sems can rnams la mya ngan las 'das pa'i lam ston gyi 'khor ba'i lam mi ston to/_/lam gol bar zhugs pa'i sems can rnams la lam srol ma dod pa ma yin pa dang /_tsher ma med pa'i lam ston gyi nyon mongs pa'i tsher mas gang ba'i lam mi ston to/_/rigs kyi bu chos bcu po de dag 
[\[81a.4\]]dang ldan na byang chub sems dpa' lam ma log par ston pa rnams yin no/_/rigs kyi bu chos bcu dang ldan na byang chub sems dpa' rtag tu rgyun mi chad par sems mnyam par gzhag pa rnams yin no/_/bcu gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_lus la dran pa shin tu nye bar gzhag cing gnas so/_/
[\[81a.5\]]tshor ba rnams dang /_sems dang chos rnams dang /_yul rnams dang /_dgon pa rnams dang /_grong dang /_grong khyer dang /_grong rdal dang /_gzhongs dang /_yul 'khor dang /_rgyal po'i pho brang 'khor rnams dang /_rnyed pa dang /_bkur sti dang /_grags pa dang /_
[\[81a.6\]]sgra dang /_tshigs su bcad pa rnams dang /_de bzhin gshegs pas bslab pa bcas pa rnams dang /_nye ba'i nyon mongs pa dang /_yongs su nyon mongs pa dang /_kun nas nyon mongs pa thams cad la dran pa shin tu nye bar gzhag cing gnas so/_/rigs kyi bu de la ji ltar na 
[\[81a.7\]]byang chub sems dpa' lus la dran pa shin tu nye bar gzhag cing gnas zhe na/_rigs kyi bu 'di la byang chub sems dpa' gang lus dang ldan pa'i sdig pa mi dge ba'i chos de dag yang dag pa'i shes rab kyis rab tu brtags shing yongs su spong ngo /_/de 'og gi rkang pa'i mthil nas brtsams te/_
[\[81b\]]
[\[81b.1\]]yar la klad pa'i bar du lus kyi nyes pa la rtog go/_/lus la bdag med par yang rtog go/_/lus mi brtan pa dang /_'jig pa dang /_chu rgyus kyis glags pa dang /_dri mi zhim pa dang /_dri nga ba dang /_kha dog mi sdug par rtog ste/_de de ltar rtog pa'i tshe/_gang lus la 
[\[81b.2\]]lus su 'dod pa dang /_lus la 'dod chags pa dang /_lus la dga' ba dang /_lus la bdag gir 'dzin pa dang /_lus la zhen pa de dag mi 'byung ste/_de'i rgyu de'i rkyen de'i gzhis lus dang ldan pa'i chos rnams kyi dbang du 'gro bar mi 'gyur te/_gzhan du na lus dang ldan pa'i 
[\[81b.3\]]chos rnams de nyid kyi dbang du 'gyur te/_de ltar na lus la dran pa shin tu nye bar gzhag cing gnas pa'o/_/ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' tshor ba rnams la dran pa shin tu nye bar gzhag cing gnas zhe na/_'di la byang chub sems dpa' 'di snyam du sems te/_tshor ba gang ji snyed pa de dag 
[\[81b.4\]]thams cad ni sdug bsngal na/_byis pa phyin ci log gis phyin ci log tu gyur pa rnams ni bde bar 'dzin te/_byis pa rmongs pa rnams sdug bsngal la bde ba zhes bya bar 'dzin gyis 'phags pa rnams kyis ni thams cad sdug bsngal bar thugs su chud do snyam nas de spang ba'i phyir nan tan du byed do/_/
[\[81b.5\]]sems can gzhan dag kyang bslab pa de nyid la 'god kyis bdag gis kyang de dag gi rjes su bslab par bya'o snyam ste/_de de ltar rtog pas tshor ba rnams la dran pa shin tu nye bar gzhag cing gnas so/_/de tshor ba dag la chags pa'ang med/_khong khro ba'ang med cing de dag spang ba'i phyir rab 
[\[81b.6\]]tu zhugs pa yin no/_/gzhan dag kyang bslab pa de nyid la 'god de/_de ltar na tshor ba rnams la dran pa shin tu nye bar gzhag cing gnas so/_/ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' sems la dran pa shin tu nye bar gzhag cing gnas zhe na/_de 'di snyam du sems te/_sems 'di ni mi rtag 
[\[81b.7\]]pa la rtag par mngon par dga' ba'o/_/sdug bsngal la bde bar mngon par dga' ba'o/_/bdag med pa la bdag tu mngon par dga' ba'o/_/mi gtsang ba la gtsang bar mngon par dga' ba'o/_/g.yo zhing mi brtan pa la myur du 'gyur ba'o/_/nyon mongs pa rnams kyi rtsa ba'o/_/

[\[82a.6\]]de dag spang ba'i phyir brtson pa yin no/_/chos 'di dag ni dge ba'o zhes bya bar shes nas chos de dag la sems gtong zhing dran pa nye bar gzhog ste/_chos de dag kyang yang dag par blangs shing gnas so/_/gzhan yang bslab pa de nyid la 'god de/_de ltar na byang chub sems dpa'

[\[82b\]]
[\[82b.1\]]la chags par 'dzin par yang mi 'gyur/_khong khro ba'i sems kyang mi skyed de/_de 'di snyam du sems te/_de ltar bdag dngos po med pa'i chos rnams la chags pa skyed pa ni bdag gi mi cha ste/_de ni bdag byis pa dang /_rmongs pa dang /_mi gsal ba dang /_mi mkhas par

[\[82b.5\]]chub sems dpa' dgon pa rnams la dran pa shin tu nye bar gzhag cing gnas zhe na/_de 'di snyam du dgon pa zhes bya ba 'di ni nyon mongs pa med par rnam par spyod pa rnams kyi gnas so/_/zhi bar rnam par spyod pa rnams kyi gnas so/_/dgon pa la ni lha dang /_klu dang /_gnod sbyin 
[\[82b.6\]]dang /_dri za gzhan gyi sems mngon par shes pa dag kyang yod de/_de dag bdag gi sems dang /_sems las byung ba'i chos rnams rab tu shes na/_bdag tshul bzhin ma yin pa'i yid la byed cing sems pa de ni bdag gi mi cha'o snyam ste/_de tshul bzhin ma yin pa yid la 
[\[82b.7\]]bya ba thams cad dang bral nas tshul bzhin du chos rnams la sems gtong zhing chos de dag la sgom pa mang ba yin te/_de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' dgon pa rnams la dran pa shin tu nye bar gzhag cing gnas so/_/ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' grong dang /_grong khyer dang /_

[\[83a.1\]]grong rdal dang /_gzhongs dang /_yul 'khor dang /_rgyal po'i pho brang 'khor rnams la dran pa shin tu nye bar gzhag cing gnas zhe na/_'di la byang chub sems dpa' grong dang /_grong khyer dang /_grong rdal dang /_gzhongs dang /_yul 'khor dang /_

[\[83a.3\]]po'i pho brang ngam/_brgyan po'i gnas sam/_ra ro ba'i 'khor gyi tshogs sam/_gar mkhan dang /_glu gar gyi gnas dang /_de ma yin pa gzhan yang rung ste/_rab tu byung ba'i tshul dang mi 'dra ba de dag yongs su spang zhing /_de dag la dran pa shin tu nye bar gzhag cing gnas te/_

[\[83a.5\]]dang /_sgra dang /_tshigs su bcad pa dag la dran pa shin tu nye bar gzhag cing gnas zhe na/_'di la byang chub sems dpa' sbyin pa byed pa rnams kyi bsod nams kyi dngos po nye bar bzung zhing /_rnyed pa dang /_bkur sti la sems gtong ste/_ci nas kyang zhen par mi 'gyur/_sred

[\[97b.1\]]dag sangs rgyas kyis yongs su bzung ba yin no/_/dge ba'i rtsa ba bskyed pa dang /_sangs rgyas mang po la bsnyen bkur byas par 'gyur te/_rigs kyi bu dang /_rigs kyi bu mo de dag gis the tsom dang /_yid gnyis dang /_ner nur mi bskyed par bya'o/_/rigs kyi bu'am/_
[\[97b.2\]]rigs kyi bu mo gang gis nub bdun du yid la bya ba gzhan med cing sems ma 'khrugs par bsam pa drag po dang /_dad pa drag pos zhugs te/_de bzhin gshegs pa'i che ba 'di rjes su sems la zhag bdun 'das pa dang des mchod pa'i cho ga nye bar gzhag nas/_gtsang bas gos gtsang 
[\[97b.3\]]ma bgos te/_dad pa mngon du byas la 'dug par byas te/_des de'i nub mo de bzhin gshegs pa mthong bar 'gyur ro/_/de ste mi byed dam cho ga ma tshang bar byed na/_de sems rtse gcig par 'gyur zhing 'chi ba'i tshe yang de bzhin gshegs pa de'i mdun na bzhugs par 'gyur ro/_/
[\[97b.4\]]byang chub sems dpa' sgrib pa thams cad rnam par sel bas gsol pa/_bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa'i che ba 'di bshad na/_sems can gang la la ma dad par 'gyur ba mchis sam/_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_rigs kyi bu de bzhin gshegs 
[\[97b.5\]]pa'i che ba 'di bshad na/_sems can gang mi bzad pa rtsub pa tsha ba'i bsam pas gnod sems bskyed par 'gyur ba dang /_chos smra ba de la yang dgrar 'du shes par 'gyur ba dag yod do/_/de dag de'i phyir lus zhig nas sems can dmyal ba dag tu skye bar 'gyur ro/_/yang 
[\[97b.6\]]rigs kyi bu sems can gang rnams de bzhin gshegs pa'i che ba bshad pa 'di la dad pa rnyed par 'gyur ba dang /_chos smra ba de la yang dge ba'i bshes gnyen du 'du shes pa dang /_ston par 'du shes skyed na/_rigs kyi bu sngon yang de bzhin gshegs pa'i che ba 'di tshe rabs de dang

[\[98a\]]
[\[98a.1\]]de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis de'i tshe ljags kyi dbang po phyung ste/_ljags kyi dbang pos zhal gyi dkyil 'khor mngon par bkab bo/_/zhal gyi dkyil 'khor mngon par bkab nas/_dbu mngon par bkab bo/_/dbu mngon par bkab nas/_sku mngon par bkab bo/_/sku mngon

[\[98a.3\]]de nas thams cad yod pa'i 'khor mngon par bkab bo/_/de nas stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams mngon par bkab bo/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis ljags kyi dbang po phyir bsdus te/_thams cad yod pa'i 'khor la bka' stsal pa/_rigs kyi bu 'di 'dra ba'i 
[\[98a.4\]]ljags kyis de bzhin gshegs pa rnams [{]brdzun [,]rdzun [}]gsung bar mi 'gyur gyi/_rigs kyi bu khyod rnams dad par gyis shig_/rigs kyi bu khyed rnams mngon par dad par gyis shig_/de ni khyed kyi yun ring po'i don dang phan pa dang /_bde bar 'gyur ro/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis 
[\[98a.5\]]bstan pa 'di bstan pa'i tshe/_byang chub sems dpa' stong phrag brgyad cu rtsa bzhis mi skye ba'i chos rnams la bzod pa thob bo/_/srog chags brgya stong du ma ni chos rnams la chos kyi mig rdul dang bral zhing dri ma med pa rnam par dag go/_/sngon sems ma bskyed pa'i sems can du mas ni byang

[\[111a.3\]]byang chub sems dpa' de dag la chos ston par snang ste/_brgya byin dang /_tshangs pa dang /_'jig rten skyong ba bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong du mas yongs su bskor cing byang chub sems dpa' bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong du mas mdun du bdar te/_'di ltar chos nyid 'di kho na brtsams nas chos ston par 
[\[111a.4\]]snang bar gyur to/_/de nas lha mo mi 'chi ba yang ting nge 'dzin de las langs te/_bcom ldan 'das la lan gsum du g.yas phyogs su bskor ba byas nas de nyid du mi snang bar gyur to/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das la byang chub sems dpa' sgrib pa thams cad rnam par sel bas 'di skad ces gsol to/_/
[\[111a.5\]]bcom ldan 'das rigs kyi bu dang /_rigs kyi bu mo gang chos kyi rnam grangs 'di nyan tam/_'dzin tam/_'chang ngam/_klog gam/_kun chub par bgyid dam/_kha ton bgyid dam/_gzhan dag la yang rgya cher yang dag par ston par bgyid na/_de bsod nams ji tsam zhig skyed par 'gyur/_bcom 
[\[111a.6\]]ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_rigs kyi bu'am/_rigs kyi bu mo gang la la zhig gis stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams su gtogs pa'i sems can rnams la sbyin pa lhug par byin te/_de ltar sbyin pas bskal pa brgya stong mang por byin pa bas/_rigs kyi bu'am/_rigs kyi bu mo gang gis 
[\[111a.7\]]chos kyi rnam grangs 'di legs par bris legs par zhus te/_sems dad pas sems dad pa la byin na/_de bas kyang 'di bsod nams ches mang du skyed do/_/rigs kyi bu de ci'i phyir zhe na/_zang zing gi sbyin pa ni phran tshegs te/_chos kyi sbyin pa ni rdzu 'phrul che ba'o/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_rigs kyi bu 'khor ba na 'khor ba'i 
[\[111b\]]
[\[111b.1\]]sems can rnams kyis zang zing ni rnam pa du mar myong gi_/'jig rten las 'das pa'i chos kyi ro ni nam yang ma myong ste/_'jig rten pa ni ma yin no/_/rigs kyi bu gang la las stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams su gtogs pa'i sems can rnams dge ba bcu'i las kyi lam rnams la bkod 
[\[111b.2\]]pa bas/_gang gis chos kyi rnam grangs 'di nyan tam/_'dzin tam/_'chang ngam/_klog gam/_kun chub par byed dam/_gzhan dag la yang rgya cher ston na/_de bas 'di bsod nams ches mang du skyed do/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_rigs kyi bu dge ba bcu'i las kyi lam rnams ni 'di las byung ngo /_/rigs 
[\[111b.3\]]kyi bu gang la la zhig gis stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams su gtogs pa'i sems can rnams rgyun du zhugs pa dang /_lan cig phyir 'ong ba dang /_phyir mi 'ong ba dang /_dgra bcom pa nyid kyi 'bras bu la bkod pa bas kyang chos kyi rnam grangs 'di nyan tam/_'dzin tam/_'chang ngam/_
[\[111b.4\]]klog gam/_kun chub par byed dam/_gzhan dag la yang rgya cher ston na/_de bas 'di bsod nams ches mang du skyed do/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_rigs kyi bu chos 'di nyid la brten na/_nyan thos dang /_rang sangs rgyas rnams snang bar 'gyur ro/_/chos 'di nyid la brten na byang chub sems dpa' rnams 
[\[111b.5\]]snang bar 'gyur ro/_/chos 'di nyid la brten na de bzhin gshegs pa rnams 'jig rten du 'byung bar 'gyur te/_rigs kyi bu chos kyi rnam grangs 'di gzung ba dang /_bklag pa dang /_spel ba dang /_kha ton byas pas mdo sde thams cad gzung ba dang /_bklag pa dang /_spel ba dang /_kha ton byas par 'gyur ro/_/
[\[111b.6\]]de ci'i phyir zhe na/_rigs kyi bu 'di ni chos thams cad kyi ma mo ste/_rigs kyi bu chos nyid 'di ma thob par chos nyid rgya chen po 'thob par mi 'gyur te/_rigs kyi bu 'di ni byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi 'dul ba'o/_/de nas sangs rgyas kyi mthus nyan thos chen po de dag thams cad stan las langs te bla 
[\[111b.7\]]gos phrag pa gcig tu gzar te/_pus mo g.yas pa'i lha nga sa la btsugs nas/_bcom ldan 'das ga la ba de logs su thal mo sbyar ba btud de/_bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to/_/bcom ldan 'das bdag cag gis kyang chos nyid 'di thos pas skye ba'i 'khor ba chen po bas par bgyis so/_/bcom ldan 
[\[112a\]]
[\[112a.1\]]'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_dge slong dag de de bzhin no/_/de de bzhin te/_ji skad smras pa de bzhin no/_/de nas yang bcom ldan 'das kyis 'khor mang po de dag la bka' stsal pa/_rigs kyi bu dag sa phyogs gang du chos kyi rnam grangs 'di 'chad pa dang /_ston pa'i sa'i phyogs de byang chub kyi snying por 
[\[112a.2\]]gzung bar bya'o/_/chos kyi 'khor lo rab tu bskor bar gzung bar bya'o/_/mchod rten chen por gzung bar bya'o/_/bdag cag gi ston pa gnas 'di na bzhugs so bar gzung bar bya'o/_/rigs kyi bu dag de ci'i phyir zhe na/_chos nyid 'di ni byang chub bo/_/chos nyid 'di ni chos kyi 'khor lo rab tu 
[\[112a.3\]]bskor ba'o/_/rigs kyi bu dag de bzhin gshegs pa ni chos kyi sku ste/_rigs kyi bu dag chos mchod pas de bzhin gshegs pa rnams mchod par 'gyur ro/_/sa phyogs gang na chos smra ba de gnas pa de la yang mchod rten du 'du shes bskyed par bya ste/_chos smra ba de la yang bla mar 'du shes bskyed par 
[\[112a.4\]]bya'o/_/dge ba'i bshes gnyen du 'du shes bskyed par bya'o/_/lam ston par 'du shes bskyed par bya'o/_/chos smra ba de mthong na dga' ba dang /_dad pa dang /_rangs pa bskyed par bya'o/_/rim gror byas te stan la bdar bar bya'o/_/chos smra ba de'i bsngags pa brjod par bya'o/_/legs so zhes brjod pa 
[\[112a.5\]]mang bar bya'o/_/rigs kyi bu dag ngas bskal pa'am/_bskal pa las lhag par chos smra ba de'i bsngags pa brjod kyang chos smra ba de la cung zad kyang phan par byas pa med do/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_rigs kyi bu dag 'di lta ste/_bkur stir bya ba dang /_btsun par bya ba dang /_bsngags pa brjod pa ni gyi na cung zad du 
[\[112a.6\]]zad do/_/rigs kyi bu dag gal te chos 'di 'dod pa'i rigs kyi bu 'am/_rigs kyi bu mo dag gis chos smra ba de 'ong ba'i lam bdag gi khrag gis chag chag byas kyang chos smra ba de la phan pa cung zad kyang byas pa med do/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_rigs kyi bu dag 'di lta ste/_de bzhin gshegs pa'i chos kyi tshul gzung 
[\[112a.7\]]ba 'di ni dka' ba'o/_/rigs kyi bu dag chos smra ba des kyang chos kyi rnam grangs 'di bshad pa dang /_bstan pa dang /_yang dag par ston pa'i tshe/_'jigs pa med par bya'o/_/ma zhum pa dang /_spa ma gong ba dang /_zhe ma 'gras pa'i sems dang /_gos gtsang ma bgos te dad pa bskyed par bya'o/_/bdag gi bsngags 
[\[112b\]]
[\[112b.1\]]pa brjod kyang mi snyems pa dang /_nga rgyal du mi gzung bar bya ste/_bdag mi stod/_gzhan mi smod cing /_des zang zing med pa'i sems kyis ri mor byas te chos bstan par bya'o/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das la lha'i dbang po brgya byin gyis 'di skad ces gsol to/_/bcom ldan 'das dog sa'i 
[\[112b.2\]]phyogs gang du chos kyi rnam grangs 'di bshad pa'am/_bstan par 'gyur ba'i sa phyogs der chos kyi rnam grangs 'di mnyan pa dang /_chos smra ba de la sman par bgyi ba'i slad du bdag cag dpung dang bcas 'khor dang bcas te mchi'o/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_kau shi ka legs so 
[\[112b.3\]]legs so/_/khyod kyis de ltar gyis shig_/kau shi ka khyod kyis de bzhin gshegs pa'i chos nyid yongs su bsrung bar bya'o/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das la byang chub sems dpa' sgrib pa thams cad rnam par sel bas gsol pa/_bcom ldan 'das chos kyi rnam grangs 'di'i ming ci zhes bgyi/_
[\[112b.4\]]'di ji ltar gzung bar bgyi/_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_rigs kyi bu de'i phyir khyod kyis chos kyi rnam grangs 'di dkon mchog sprin ces bya bar zung shig_/yon tan rin po che'i 'byung gnas kyi le'u zhes bya bar yang zung shig_/ye shes sgron ma zhes bya bar yang zung shig_/sgrib pa thams 
[\[112b.5\]]cad rnam par sel bas kun dris pa zhes bya bar yang zung shig_/bcom ldan 'das kyis de skad ces bka' stsal pa dang /_byang chub sems dpa' sgrib pa thams cad rnam par sel ba yid dga' zhing byang chub sems dpa' de dag dang /_nyan thos chen po de dag dang /_brgya byin dang /_tshangs pa dang /_
[\[112b.6\]]'jig rten skyong ba dang /_dbang phyug chen po dang /_gnas gtsang ma'i lha'i bu la sogs pa/_lha'i bu brgya stong du ma dag dang /_de ma yin pa gzhan lha dang /_klu dang /_gnod sbyin dang /_dri za dang /_lha ma yin dang /_nam mkha' lding dang /_mi 'am ci dang /_lto 'phye chen po brgya stong dag kyang bcom ldan 'das 
[\[112b.7\]]kyis gsungs pa la mngon par dga'o/_/'phags pa dkon mchog sprin ces bya ba theg pa chen po'i mdo rdzogs so//_//lo ts+tsha ba ban de rin chen 'tsho dang /_chos nyid tshul khrims kyis bsgyur cing zhus te gtan la phab pa//_//
